user
Ты ревьюишь волны 2 («Read») и 3 («Edit») проекта Plain — личного Markdown reader/editor для Windows (Tauri 2 + React 19 + TS + Vite + CodeMirror 6). Ничего не правь — только читай и пиши отзыв.

Контекст: личный проект для одного человека; норма — `ТЗ-Plain.md` v2.2 (§1.3, §5.0–5.3, §9, §11, §12, §14, §16), дизайн — `research/00-design-system-from-mockups.md`, макеты — `mockups/Markdown Editor.dc.html` (1a/1b read, 1c edit). Код волны 2: `src/read/**`, `src/ui/read.css`, `src/ui/Outline.tsx`. Волны 3: `src/editor/**`, `src/ui/editor.css`. Волны 1, 4, 5 (`src/app/**`, `src/library/**`, `src-tauri/**`) уже приняты — только контекст. Приложение запущено живьём и работает; тесты зелёные (174 vitest).

Что ищем, по приоритету:
1. Баги и тихие потери данных на стыке read/edit: `Doc.text` в zustand vs `EditorState` (sync.ts: idle-flush текста, rAF-каретка, `flushActiveEditor`), нормализация CRLF→LF в буфере и `savedText` (buffers.ts), события `plain:goto-line`/`plain:goto-heading`, переключение документов при открытом редакторе, утечки слушателей/буферов.
2. Безопасность §14 в пайплайне read (`src/read/pipeline.ts`): схема rehype-sanitize (`clobber: []`, добавленные атрибуты/классы, `dataSrc`), KaTeX после sanitize (`raw` узлы), `rehype-raw`, обработка ссылок (`javascript:`, `file:`), `convertFileSrc`/asset scope (`assets.ts`, `allow_asset_dir` в lib.rs расширяет и fs-scope на всю папку).
3. Корректность markdown: micromark-расширение wikilinks (`wikilink.ts`), callouts как mdast-трансформ (`callout.ts`), guard против валюты в `$` (`remarkMathGuard`), word count.
4. Команды форматирования (`src/editor/commands.ts`): toggle-семантика, мультивыделение, границы; `codeKeymap` в capture-фазе по `KeyboardEvent.code` (keymap.ts) — конфликты со стандартным keymap CM6 (например `Ctrl+D`, `Tab`), IME.
5. Производительность на 1 МБ: что на горячем пути ввода (setup.ts `lineLook` декорации, `frontMatterEnd`, `inCode` через syntaxTree), `render()` синхронный в useMemo.
6. Оверинжиниринг — критерий «окупится ли за месяц использования одним человеком».

Формат: список находок, каждая — файл:строка, суть, почему важно, предложение. Раздели на «критично / стоит поправить / вкусовщина». Не пересказывай код, не хвали. По-русски, термины по-английски.

"C:\\WINDOWS\\System32\\WindowsPowerShell\\v1.0\\powershell.exe" -Command '$paths = @('"'src/editor/sync.ts','src/editor/buffers.ts','src/editor/EditView.tsx','src/editor/setup.ts','src/editor/keymap.ts','src/editor/remote.ts','src/editor/commands.ts'); foreach ("'$p in $paths) { Write-Output "`nFILE $p"; $n=0; Get-Content -LiteralPath $p -Encoding UTF8 | ForEach-Object { $n++; '"'{0}: {1}' -f "'$n,$_ } }' in G:\Claude Projects\md reader

FILE src/editor/sync.ts
1: // Editor -> store. Nothing is written from inside a CodeMirror update:
2: // measured on a 1 MB file, a store write on that path cost ~30 ms per
3: // keystroke and 2 ms off it. So the caret rides one frame behind and the text
4: // goes back when the browser is idle (the status bar counts words over it).
5: 
6: import { EditorView } from "@codemirror/view";
7: import type { EditorState } from "@codemirror/state";
8: import type { Doc } from "../app/store";
9: import { useStore } from "../app/store";
10: import { isDirty } from "./buffers";
11: 
12: export function caretOf(state: EditorState): { line: number; col: number } {
13:   const head = state.selection.main.head;
14:   const line = state.doc.lineAt(head);
15:   return { line: line.number, col: head - line.from + 1 };
16: }
17: 
18: let idle: number | null = null;
19: let frame: number | null = null;
20: 
21: function cancelIdle(): void {
22:   if (idle === null) return;
23:   if (typeof window.cancelIdleCallback === "function") window.cancelIdleCallback(idle);
24:   else window.clearTimeout(idle);
25:   idle = null;
26: }
27: 
28: function cancelFrame(): void {
29:   if (frame === null) return;
30:   cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
31:   frame = null;
32: }
33: 
34: /**
35:  * Writes text and caret into the store now - on idle, on unmount and on a
36:  * document switch. It takes the caret too, because it cancels the frame that
37:  * would otherwise have carried it.
38:  */
39: export function flushText(view: EditorView, id: string): void {
40:   cancelIdle();
41:   cancelFrame();
42:   const store = useStore.getState();
43:   const current = store.docs.find((d) => d.id === id);
44:   if (!current) return;
45:   const text = view.state.doc.toString();
46:   const caret = caretOf(view.state);
47:   const patch: Partial<Omit<Doc, "id">> = {};
48:   if (current.text !== text) patch.text = text;
49:   // The authority on `dirty`: the frame below only ever guesses `true`, and
50:   // an undo or a reload from disk has to be able to take it back.
51:   const dirty = isDirty(id, text);
52:   if (current.dirty !== dirty) patch.dirty = dirty;
53:   const previous = current.caret;
54:   if (!previous || previous.line !== caret.line || previous.col !== caret.col) {
55:     patch.caret = caret;
56:   }
57:   if (Object.keys(patch).length > 0) store.updateDoc(id, patch);
58: }
59: 
60: function scheduleText(view: EditorView, id: string): void {
61:   if (idle !== null) return;
62:   const run = () => {
63:     idle = null;
64:     flushText(view, id);
65:   };
66:   idle =
67:     typeof window.requestIdleCallback === "function"
68:       ? window.requestIdleCallback(run, { timeout: 500 })
69:       : window.setTimeout(run, 200);
70: }
71: 
72: /** Caret, plus the dirty dot - it shouldn't wait for the text flush. */
73: function pushCaret(view: EditorView, id: string, dirty: boolean): void {
74:   const store = useStore.getState();
75:   const current = store.docs.find((d) => d.id === id);
76:   if (!current) return;
77:   const caret = caretOf(view.state);
78:   const previous = current.caret;
79:   const patch: Partial<Omit<Doc, "id">> = {};
80:   if (!previous || previous.line !== caret.line || previous.col !== caret.col) {
81:     patch.caret = caret;
82:   }
83:   if (dirty && !current.dirty) patch.dirty = true;
84:   if (Object.keys(patch).length > 0) store.updateDoc(id, patch);
85: }
86: 
87: /** On mount and on document switch the status bar should not lag a frame. */
88: export function syncCaret(view: EditorView, id: string): void {
89:   cancelFrame();
90:   pushCaret(view, id, false);
91: }
92: 
93: function scheduleCaret(view: EditorView, id: string, dirty: boolean): void {
94:   if (frame !== null) return;
95:   frame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
96:     frame = null;
97:     pushCaret(view, id, dirty);
98:   });
99: }
100: 
101: /** The editor always shows the active document, so that is the id to patch. */
102: export const storeSync = EditorView.updateListener.of((update) => {
103:   if (!update.docChanged && !update.selectionSet) return;
104:   const id = useStore.getState().activeId;
105:   if (id === null) return;
106:   if (update.docChanged) scheduleText(update.view, id);
107:   scheduleCaret(update.view, id, update.docChanged);
108: });

FILE src/editor/buffers.ts
1: // One EditorState per document, kept outside React so that leaving edit and
2: // coming back keeps history, selection and scroll (spec 5.0).
3: 
4: import { EditorState } from "@codemirror/state";
5: import { normalizeEol } from "../app/eol";
6: import type { Doc } from "../app/store";
7: import { useStore } from "../app/store";
8: import type { Settings } from "../app/settings";
9: import { editorExtensions } from "./setup";
10: 
11: export interface Buffer {
12:   state: EditorState;
13:   /**
14:    * The text on disk as CodeMirror holds it - line endings normalized to
15:    * `\n`. `dirty` is a comparison against this, so both sides must be
16:    * normalized or a CRLF file would never look clean again. Saving writes
17:    * the file back in its own dominant style (8).
18:    */
19:   savedText: string;
20:   scrollTop: number;
21: }
22: 
23: const buffers = new Map<string, Buffer>();
24: 
25: /** The buffer for a document, created from `doc.text` on first use. */
26: export function buffer(doc: Doc, settings: Settings): Buffer {
27:   const existing = buffers.get(doc.id);
28:   if (existing) return existing;
29:   const state = EditorState.create({
30:     doc: doc.text,
31:     extensions: editorExtensions(doc, settings),
32:   });
33:   // Not the text we just loaded: a restored draft is already unsaved work,
34:   // and its clean text is whatever the file on disk holds (spec 8).
35:   const created: Buffer = {
36:     state,
37:     savedText: normalizeEol(doc.savedText),
38:     scrollTop: 0,
39:   };
40:   buffers.set(doc.id, created);
41:   return created;
42: }
43: 
44: export function keepBuffer(id: string, state: EditorState, scrollTop: number): void {
45:   const existing = buffers.get(id);
46:   if (existing) {
47:     existing.state = state;
48:     existing.scrollTop = scrollTop;
49:   }
50: }
51: 
52: /** Called after a successful save, with the text that reached the disk. */
53: export function markSaved(id: string, text: string): void {
54:   const existing = buffers.get(id);
55:   if (existing) existing.savedText = normalizeEol(text);
56: }
57: 
58: /**
59:  * The one dirty test. A document that has never been in edit has no buffer,
60:  * so the document's own copy of the disk text answers for it.
61:  */
62: export function isDirty(id: string, text: string): boolean {
63:   const existing = buffers.get(id);
64:   if (existing) return existing.savedText !== normalizeEol(text);
65:   const doc = useStore.getState().docs.find((d) => d.id === id);
66:   return doc ? normalizeEol(doc.savedText) !== normalizeEol(text) : false;
67: }
68: 
69: /** An external reload, applied as one change so undo has a single step. */
70: export function setBufferText(id: string, text: string): void {
71:   const existing = buffers.get(id);
72:   if (!existing) return;
73:   existing.state = existing.state.update({
74:     changes: { from: 0, to: existing.state.doc.length, insert: text },
75:   }).state;
76: }
77: 
78: /** Save As keeps the buffer - history and all - under the new file's id. */
79: export function moveBuffer(from: string, to: string): void {
80:   const existing = buffers.get(from);
81:   if (!existing || from === to) return;
82:   buffers.delete(from);
83:   buffers.set(to, existing);
84: }
85: 
86: export function dropBuffer(id: string): void {
87:   buffers.delete(id);
88: }
89: 
90: // A closed document must not leave its buffer behind: opening the file again
91: // has to read it from disk, not from a state we kept.
92: useStore.subscribe((state, previous) => {
93:   if (state.docs.length >= previous.docs.length) return;
94:   for (const id of buffers.keys()) {
95:     if (!state.docs.some((d) => d.id === id)) buffers.delete(id);
96:   }
97: });
98: 
99: /** Tests only. */
100: export function clearBuffers(): void {
101:   buffers.clear();
102: }

FILE src/editor/EditView.tsx
1: import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
2: import { EditorView, type Command as EditorCommand } from "@codemirror/view";
3: import type { Doc } from "../app/store";
4: import { useStore } from "../app/store";
5: import { buffer, keepBuffer, markSaved, moveBuffer, setBufferText } from "./buffers";
6: import { headingAt } from "./headings";
7: import { gutterConf, gutterExtension, lineNumbersOn, onLineNumbers } from "./setup";
8: import { flushText, syncCaret } from "./sync";
9: import "../ui/editor.css";
10: 
11: const GOTO_LINE = "plain:goto-line";
12: const GOTO_HEADING = "plain:goto-heading";
13: /** A jump sent just before this view mounts is still ours; an older one is not. */
14: const PENDING_MS = 2000;
15: 
16: let live: EditorView | null = null;
17: let liveId: string | null = null;
18: let pending: { line: number; at: number } | null = null;
19: 
20: /**
21:  * Puts whatever the editor holds into the store synchronously. Wave 4 calls
22:  * this before saving, so the snapshot never waits for the idle callback.
23:  */
24: export function flushActiveEditor(): void {
25:   if (live && liveId) flushText(live, liveId);
26: }
27: 
28: /**
29:  * Runs a CodeMirror command against whichever editor is on screen. The menu
30:  * bar needs this: undo, bold and friends are editor commands, and the menu
31:  * has no view of its own (spec 4). Nothing mounted - nothing happens.
32:  */
33: export function runEditorCommand(command: EditorCommand): boolean {
34:   if (!live) return false;
35:   // The menu took the focus away; the command works on the selection anyway,
36:   // but the caret has to come back or the next keystroke goes nowhere.
37:   live.focus();
38:   return command(live);
39: }
40: 
41: /**
42:  * Replaces the whole document in one transaction, so a silent reload from
43:  * disk is a single undo step (spec 8). The new text becomes the clean one.
44:  */
45: export function replaceText(id: string, text: string): void {
46:   if (live && liveId === id) {
47:     live.dispatch({ changes: { from: 0, to: live.state.doc.length, insert: text } });
48:     keepBuffer(id, live.state, live.scrollDOM.scrollTop);
49:     markSaved(id, text);
50:     // The dispatch queued a sync that would report the document as edited;
51:     // running it now, after the new text is the clean one, settles it.
52:     flushText(live, id);
53:     return;
54:   }
55:   setBufferText(id, text);
56:   markSaved(id, text);
57: }
58: 
59: /**
60:  * Save As: the buffer follows the document to its new id. Whatever the live
61:  * editor holds is stored first, or the move would carry a stale state.
62:  */
63: export function renameBuffer(from: string, to: string): void {
64:   if (live && liveId === from) {
65:     keepBuffer(from, live.state, live.scrollDOM.scrollTop);
66:     liveId = to;
67:   }
68:   moveBuffer(from, to);
69: }
70: 
71: function jump(view: EditorView, line: number): void {
72:   const target = view.state.doc.line(Math.min(Math.max(1, line), view.state.doc.lines));
73:   // Focus first: focusing after the transaction makes the browser scroll to
74:   // where the selection used to be, undoing the jump.
75:   view.focus();
76:   view.dispatch({
77:     selection: { anchor: target.from },
78:     effects: EditorView.scrollIntoView(target.from, { y: "start" }),
79:   });
80: }
81: 
82: // The outline and read -> edit send this; it can arrive before the view is
83: // mounted, so it waits here until it is. The listener lives on the module so
84: // it is there before the first mount; the window slot keeps a hot reload from
85: // leaving the previous module's listener (and its dead view) behind.
86: const LISTENER_SLOT = "__plainGotoLine";
87: 
88: if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
89:   const slot = window as unknown as Record<string, EventListener | undefined>;
90:   const previous = slot[LISTENER_SLOT];
91:   if (previous) window.removeEventListener(GOTO_LINE, previous);
92:   const onGotoLine: EventListener = (event) => {
93:     const line = (event as CustomEvent<number>).detail;
94:     if (typeof line !== "number") return;
95:     if (live) jump(live, line);
96:     else pending = { line, at: Date.now() };
97:   };
98:   slot[LISTENER_SLOT] = onGotoLine;
99:   window.addEventListener(GOTO_LINE, onGotoLine);
100: }
101: 
102: function takePending(view: EditorView): void {
103:   if (pending && Date.now() - pending.at < PENDING_MS) jump(view, pending.line);
104:   pending = null;
105: }
106: 
107: /** Leaving edit: tell read which heading the top of the screen was under. */
108: function announceHeading(view: EditorView, id: string): void {
109:   const store = useStore.getState();
110:   const doc = store.docs.find((d) => d.id === id);
111:   if (store.activeId !== id || doc?.mode !== "read") return;
112:   let line = 1;
113:   try {
114:     const top = view.scrollDOM.getBoundingClientRect().top - view.documentTop;
115:     line = view.state.doc.lineAt(view.lineBlockAtHeight(top).from).number;
116:   } catch {
117:     return;
118:   }
119:   const heading = headingAt(view.state.doc.toString(), line);
120:   if (!heading) return;
121:   // After the current commit, so the read view is already listening.
122:   window.setTimeout(() => {
123:     window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(GOTO_HEADING, { detail: heading.id }));
124:   }, 0);
125: }
126: 
127: export function EditView({ doc }: { doc: Doc }) {
128:   const host = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
129:   const view = useRef<EditorView | null>(null);
130:   const shown = useRef(doc.id);
131:   const [numbers, setNumbers] = useState(lineNumbersOn);
132: 
133:   useEffect(() => onLineNumbers(() => setNumbers(lineNumbersOn())), []);
134: 
135:   useEffect(() => {
136:     const parent = host.current;
137:     if (!parent) return;
138:     const { settings } = useStore.getState();
139:     const opened = buffer(doc, settings);
140:     const created = new EditorView({ state: opened.state, parent });
141:     view.current = created;
142:     live = created;
143:     liveId = doc.id;
144:     shown.current = doc.id;
145:     syncCaret(created, doc.id);
146:     // Focus first: focusing scrolls the caret into view, so anything we do
147:     // to the scroll position has to come after it.
148:     created.focus();
149:     created.scrollDOM.scrollTop = opened.scrollTop;
150:     takePending(created);
151: 
152:     return () => {
153:       const id = shown.current;
154:       flushText(created, id);
155:       keepBuffer(id, created.state, created.scrollDOM.scrollTop);
156:       announceHeading(created, id);
157:       live = null;
158:       liveId = null;
159:       view.current = null;
160:       created.destroy();
161:     };
162:     // Mount only: switching documents is handled below, on the same view.
163:   }, []);
164: 
165:   // Another document became active while we stay in edit.
166:   useEffect(() => {
167:     const current = view.current;
168:     if (!current || shown.current === doc.id) return;
169:     const previous = shown.current;
170:     flushText(current, previous);
171:     keepBuffer(previous, current.state, current.scrollDOM.scrollTop);
172: 
173:     const next = buffer(doc, useStore.getState().settings);
174:     current.setState(next.state);
175:     current.dispatch({ effects: gutterConf.reconfigure(gutterExtension(lineNumbersOn())) });
176:     shown.current = doc.id;
177:     liveId = doc.id;
178:     syncCaret(current, doc.id);
179:     current.focus();
180:     current.scrollDOM.scrollTop = next.scrollTop;
181:     takePending(current);
182:   }, [doc.id]);
183: 
184:   useEffect(() => {
185:     view.current?.dispatch({ effects: gutterConf.reconfigure(gutterExtension(numbers)) });
186:   }, [numbers]);
187: 
188:   return <div className={"edit" + (numbers ? "" : " edit-no-gutter")} ref={host} />;
189: }

FILE src/editor/setup.ts
1: // One place that assembles the CodeMirror extensions. The only compartment
2: // is the line-number gutter, which Ctrl+Shift+9 flips while the app runs.
3: 
4: import { closeBrackets, closeBracketsKeymap } from "@codemirror/autocomplete";
5: import { defaultKeymap, history, historyKeymap } from "@codemirror/commands";
6: import {
7:   deleteMarkupBackward,
8:   insertNewlineContinueMarkupCommand,
9:   markdown,
10:   markdownLanguage,
11: } from "@codemirror/lang-markdown";
12: import { languages } from "@codemirror/language-data";
13: import { codeFolding, indentUnit, syntaxHighlighting, syntaxTree } from "@codemirror/language";
14: import type { SyntaxNode } from "@lezer/common";
15: import { search, searchKeymap } from "@codemirror/search";
16: import {
17:   Compartment,
18:   EditorState,
19:   Prec,
20:   RangeSetBuilder,
21:   countColumn,
22:   type Extension,
23: } from "@codemirror/state";
24: import {
25:   Decoration,
26:   EditorView,
27:   ViewPlugin,
28:   drawSelection,
29:   dropCursor,
30:   keymap,
31:   lineNumbers,
32:   type DecorationSet,
33:   type ViewUpdate,
34: } from "@codemirror/view";
35: import type { Settings } from "../app/settings";
36: import type { Doc } from "../app/store";
37: import { useStore } from "../app/store";
38: import { detectIndent } from "./commands";
39: import { editorKeymap } from "./keymap";
40: import { findPanel } from "./findPanel";
41: import { remoteCommands } from "./remote";
42: import { storeSync } from "./sync";
43: import { formatToolbar } from "./toolbar";
44: import { editorTheme, highlightStyle } from "./theme";
45: 
46: export const gutterConf = new Compartment();
47: 
48: /* ------------------------------------------------- soft wrap continuation */
49: 
50: // Leading blockquote/list markup, so a wrapped line lines up with its text.
51: const MARKUP = /^(?:[ \t]*(?:>[ \t]?|[-*+][ \t]+|\d+[.)][ \t]+))*[ \t]*/;
52: 
53: /** Last line of a leading `---` block; 0 when the file has no front matter. */
54: function frontMatterEnd(state: EditorState): number {
55:   if (state.doc.lines < 2 || !/^---\s*$/.test(state.doc.line(1).text)) return 0;
56:   const limit = Math.min(state.doc.lines, 200);
57:   for (let n = 2; n <= limit; n += 1) {
58:     if (/^(---|\.\.\.)\s*$/.test(state.doc.line(n).text)) return n;
59:   }
60:   return 0;
61: }
62: 
63: /** Fenced code, so the whole block gets one background like read does. */
64: function inCode(state: EditorState, pos: number): boolean {
65:   for (let node: SyntaxNode | null = syntaxTree(state).resolveInner(pos, 1); node; node = node.parent) {
66:     if (node.name === "FencedCode" || node.name === "CodeBlock") return true;
67:   }
68:   return false;
69: }
70: 
71: function lineDecorations(view: EditorView): DecorationSet {
72:   const builder = new RangeSetBuilder<Decoration>();
73:   const { state } = view;
74:   const frontMatter = frontMatterEnd(state);
75:   for (const { from, to } of view.visibleRanges) {
76:     for (let pos = from; pos <= to; ) {
77:       const line = state.doc.lineAt(pos);
78:       const markup = MARKUP.exec(line.text)?.[0] ?? "";
79:       const width = markup ? Math.min(countColumn(markup, state.tabSize), 24) : 0;
80:       const cls =
81:         line.number <= frontMatter
82:           ? "cm-frontmatter"
83:           : inCode(state, line.from)
84:             ? "cm-code"
85:             : "";
86:       if (width > 0 || cls) {
87:         builder.add(
88:           line.from,
89:           line.from,
90:           Decoration.line({
91:             class: cls || undefined,
92:             attributes:
93:               width > 0
94:                 ? { style: `text-indent:-${width}ch;padding-left:${width}ch` }
95:                 : undefined,
96:           }),
97:         );
98:       }
99:       pos = line.to + 1;
100:     }
101:   }
102:   return builder.finish();
103: }
104: 
105: const lineLook = ViewPlugin.fromClass(
106:   class {
107:     decorations: DecorationSet;
108:     constructor(view: EditorView) {
109:       this.decorations = lineDecorations(view);
110:     }
111:     update(update: ViewUpdate) {
112:       if (update.docChanged || update.viewportChanged) {
113:         this.decorations = lineDecorations(update.view);
114:       }
115:     }
116:   },
117:   { decorations: (v) => v.decorations },
118: );
119: 
120: /* ----------------------------------------------------- line numbers (12) */
121: 
122: // Ctrl+Shift+9 flips them for the session; settings.json is the starting point.
123: let numbersOverride: boolean | null = null;
124: const numbersListeners = new Set<() => void>();
125: 
126: export function lineNumbersOn(): boolean {
127:   return numbersOverride ?? useStore.getState().settings.edit.lineNumbers;
128: }
129: 
130: export function toggleLineNumbers(): void {
131:   numbersOverride = !lineNumbersOn();
132:   for (const listener of numbersListeners) listener();
133: }
134: 
135: export function onLineNumbers(listener: () => void): () => void {
136:   numbersListeners.add(listener);
137:   return () => numbersListeners.delete(listener);
138: }
139: 
140: /**
141:  * The settings screen moved the base, so the session override is stale - the
142:  * setting is what the user just looked at (spec 10).
143:  */
144: export function syncLineNumbers(): void {
145:   numbersOverride = null;
146:   for (const listener of numbersListeners) listener();
147: }
148: 
149: export function gutterExtension(on: boolean): Extension {
150:   return on ? lineNumbers() : [];
151: }
152: 
153: /* ------------------------------------------------------------- assembling */
154: 
155: const reducedMotion =
156:   typeof window !== "undefined" &&
157:   window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;
158: 
159: // Enter continues the list, the quote or the checkbox; on an empty item it
160: // takes a level off instead of loosening the list (spec 5.2).
161: const markupKeymap = Prec.high(
162:   keymap.of([
163:     { key: "Enter", run: insertNewlineContinueMarkupCommand({ nonTightLists: false }) },
164:     { key: "Backspace", run: deleteMarkupBackward },
165:   ]),
166: );
167: 
168: /** No language on big files: no parse, no highlighting (spec 8). */
169: function languageExtension(large: boolean): Extension {
170:   if (large) return [];
171:   return [
172:     markdown({ base: markdownLanguage, codeLanguages: languages, addKeymap: false }),
173:     markupKeymap,
174:   ];
175: }
176: 
177: export function editorExtensions(doc: Doc, settings: Settings): Extension {
178:   const unit = settings.edit.indentUnit === "tab" ? "\t" : " ".repeat(settings.edit.indentUnit);
179:   return [
180:     editorTheme,
181:     syntaxHighlighting(highlightStyle),
182:     history(),
183:     drawSelection({ cursorBlinkRate: reducedMotion ? 0 : 1100 }),
184:     dropCursor(),
185:     EditorState.allowMultipleSelections.of(true),
186:     EditorView.clickAddsSelectionRange.of((event) => event.altKey),
187:     EditorState.readOnly.of(doc.readOnly),
188:     // Microcopy is lowercase latin (spec 1.4); this is CodeMirror's own.
189:     EditorState.phrases.of({ "Go to line": "go to line", go: "go", close: "?" }),
190:     EditorView.lineWrapping,
191:     lineLook,
192:     codeFolding(),
193:     closeBrackets(),
194:     search({ top: true, createPanel: findPanel }),
195:     gutterConf.of(gutterExtension(lineNumbersOn())),
196:     indentUnit.of(detectIndent(doc.text, unit)),
197:     languageExtension(doc.large),
198:     storeSync,
199:     remoteCommands,
200:     formatToolbar,
201:     editorKeymap(),
202:     keymap.of([...closeBracketsKeymap, ...historyKeymap, ...searchKeymap, ...defaultKeymap]),
203:   ];
204: }

FILE src/editor/keymap.ts
1: // CodeMirror matches key bindings by `key`, which breaks every letter chord
2: // in a Russian layout (spec 12). Our commands go through this thin layer
3: // instead: same chord spelling as the app, matched by KeyboardEvent.code.
4: 
5: import {
6:   deleteLine,
7:   indentLess,
8:   indentMore,
9:   redo,
10:   selectAll,
11:   undo,
12: } from "@codemirror/commands";
13: import { foldCode, unfoldCode } from "@codemirror/language";
14: import { selectNextOccurrence } from "@codemirror/search";
15: import type { Extension } from "@codemirror/state";
16: import { ViewPlugin, type Command } from "@codemirror/view";
17: import { matchesChord, parseChord } from "../app/chords";
18: import {
19:   cycleList,
20:   insertLink,
21:   tabIndent,
22:   toggleBold,
23:   toggleCheckbox,
24:   toggleCodeBlock,
25:   toggleHeading,
26:   toggleInlineCode,
27:   toggleItalic,
28:   toggleQuote,
29: } from "./commands";
30: 
31: export interface CodeBinding {
32:   chord: string;
33:   run: Command;
34: }
35: 
36: /**
37:  * Runs the first binding whose physical chord matches. This listens on the
38:  * editor in the capture phase rather than through CodeMirror's own keydown
39:  * handling, because the app swallows Ctrl+F/Ctrl+G with preventDefault on
40:  * window (spec 12) and CodeMirror skips its handlers once that is set.
41:  */
42: export function codeKeymap(bindings: CodeBinding[]): Extension {
43:   const compiled = bindings.map((b) => ({ chord: parseChord(b.chord), run: b.run }));
44:   return ViewPlugin.define((view) => {
45:     const onKeydown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
46:       if (event.isComposing) return;
47:       // The find panel has its own keys.
48:       if ((event.target as HTMLElement | null)?.closest(".cm-panels")) return;
49:       for (const binding of compiled) {
50:         if (!matchesChord(binding.chord, event)) continue;
51:         if (binding.run(view)) event.preventDefault();
52:         return;
53:       }
54:     };
55:     view.dom.addEventListener("keydown", onKeydown, true);
56:     return { destroy: () => view.dom.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeydown, true) };
57:   });
58: }
59: 
60: /** Chords the app layer owns; CodeMirror must keep its hands off them. */
61: const yieldToApp: Command = () => true;
62: 
63: export function editorKeymap(): Extension {
64:   return codeKeymap([
65:     // formatting (5.2)
66:     { chord: "Ctrl+B", run: toggleBold },
67:     { chord: "Ctrl+I", run: toggleItalic },
68:     { chord: "Ctrl+E", run: toggleInlineCode },
69:     { chord: "Ctrl+Shift+`", run: toggleCodeBlock },
70:     { chord: "Ctrl+Shift+K", run: insertLink },
71:     { chord: "Ctrl+Shift+Q", run: toggleQuote },
72:     { chord: "Ctrl+Shift+L", run: cycleList },
73:     { chord: "Ctrl+Enter", run: toggleCheckbox },
74:     { chord: "Ctrl+1", run: toggleHeading(1) },
75:     { chord: "Ctrl+2", run: toggleHeading(2) },
76:     { chord: "Ctrl+3", run: toggleHeading(3) },
77:     { chord: "Ctrl+4", run: toggleHeading(4) },
78:     { chord: "Ctrl+5", run: toggleHeading(5) },
79:     { chord: "Ctrl+6", run: toggleHeading(6) },
80:     { chord: "Ctrl+]", run: indentMore },
81:     { chord: "Ctrl+[", run: indentLess },
82:     { chord: "Tab", run: tabIndent },
83:     { chord: "Shift+Tab", run: indentLess },
84: 
85:     // editing (12)
86:     { chord: "Ctrl+Z", run: undo },
87:     { chord: "Ctrl+Shift+Z", run: redo },
88:     { chord: "Ctrl+Y", run: redo },
89:     { chord: "Ctrl+A", run: selectAll },
90:     { chord: "Ctrl+D", run: selectNextOccurrence },
91:     { chord: "Ctrl+Shift+X", run: deleteLine },
92:     { chord: "Ctrl+Shift+[", run: foldCode },
93:     { chord: "Ctrl+Shift+]", run: unfoldCode },
94: 
95:     // Find, replace and go-to-line are app commands now: the registry owns
96:     // their chords and remote.ts answers the events (spec 12).
97: 
98:     // Ctrl+/ is the app's read/edit switch, not CodeMirror's comment toggle.
99:     { chord: "Ctrl+/", run: yieldToApp },
100:   ]);
101: }

FILE src/editor/remote.ts
1: // Find, replace and `go to line` are app commands (spec 12), so their chords
2: // live in the registry and the editor only answers the events. Without this
3: // the same chord would be handled twice: once by CodeMirror, once by the app.
4: 
5: import { findNext, findPrevious, gotoLine, openSearchPanel } from "@codemirror/search";
6: import type { Extension } from "@codemirror/state";
7: import { ViewPlugin } from "@codemirror/view";
8: import { FIND, FIND_STEP, GOTO_LINE_PROMPT, REPLACE } from "../read/events";
9: import { openReplace } from "./findPanel";
10: 
11: export const remoteCommands: Extension = ViewPlugin.define((view) => {
12:   const find = () => openSearchPanel(view);
13:   const replace = () => openReplace(view);
14:   const step = (event: Event) => {
15:     const direction = (event as CustomEvent<1 | -1>).detail;
16:     if (direction < 0) findPrevious(view);
17:     else findNext(view);
18:   };
19:   const goto = () => gotoLine(view);
20: 
21:   window.addEventListener(FIND, find);
22:   window.addEventListener(REPLACE, replace);
23:   window.addEventListener(FIND_STEP, step);
24:   window.addEventListener(GOTO_LINE_PROMPT, goto);
25: 
26:   return {
27:     destroy() {
28:       window.removeEventListener(FIND, find);
29:       window.removeEventListener(REPLACE, replace);
30:       window.removeEventListener(FIND_STEP, step);
31:       window.removeEventListener(GOTO_LINE_PROMPT, goto);
32:     },
33:   };
34: });

FILE src/editor/commands.ts
1: // Markdown formatting commands (spec 5.2). All of them are plain text
2: // operations over the selection or the line; applying one twice undoes it.
3: 
4: import { indentMore } from "@codemirror/commands";
5: import {
6:   EditorSelection,
7:   type ChangeSpec,
8:   type EditorState,
9:   type Line,
10:   type StateCommand,
11: } from "@codemirror/state";
12: import { indentUnit } from "@codemirror/language";
13: import type { Command } from "@codemirror/view";
14: 
15: /** Every line touched by the selection, once, in document order. */
16: export function selectedLines(state: EditorState): Line[] {
17:   const lines: Line[] = [];
18:   let seen = 0;
19:   for (const range of state.selection.ranges) {
20:     let pos = range.from;
21:     for (;;) {
22:       const line = state.doc.lineAt(pos);
23:       if (line.number > seen) {
24:         lines.push(line);
25:         seen = line.number;
26:       }
27:       if (line.to >= range.to) break;
28:       pos = line.to + 1;
29:     }
30:   }
31:   return lines;
32: }
33: 
34: /* ---------------------------------------------------------------- inline */
35: 
36: const BOLD = "**";
37: const ITALIC = "*";
38: const CODE = "`";
39: const STRIKE = "~~";
40: 
41: /** True when a `*` next to the range is really half of a `**`. */
42: function partOfBold(state: EditorState, from: number, to: number): boolean {
43:   return (
44:     state.sliceDoc(Math.max(0, from - 2), Math.max(0, from - 1)) === "*" ||
45:     state.sliceDoc(Math.min(state.doc.length, to + 1), Math.min(state.doc.length, to + 2)) === "*"
46:   );
47: }
48: 
49: function toggleInline(mark: string): StateCommand {
50:   const n = mark.length;
51:   return ({ state, dispatch }) => {
52:     const tr = state.changeByRange((range) => {
53:       const inner = state.sliceDoc(range.from, range.to);
54:       const before = state.sliceDoc(Math.max(0, range.from - n), range.from);
55:       const after = state.sliceDoc(range.to, Math.min(state.doc.length, range.to + n));
56:       const bold = mark === ITALIC && partOfBold(state, range.from, range.to);
57: 
58:       // Marks sit just outside the selection: take them off.
59:       if (before === mark && after === mark && !bold) {
60:         return {
61:           changes: [
62:             { from: range.from - n, to: range.from },
63:             { from: range.to, to: range.to + n },
64:           ],
65:           range: EditorSelection.range(range.from - n, range.to - n),
66:         };
67:       }
68:       // Marks are inside the selection: take them off.
69:       if (
70:         inner.length >= 2 * n &&
71:         inner.startsWith(mark) &&
72:         inner.endsWith(mark) &&
73:         !(mark === ITALIC && inner.startsWith(BOLD))
74:       ) {
75:         return {
76:           changes: [
77:             { from: range.from, to: range.from + n },
78:             { from: range.to - n, to: range.to },
79:           ],
80:           range: EditorSelection.range(range.from, range.to - 2 * n),
81:         };
82:       }
83:       return {
84:         changes: { from: range.from, to: range.to, insert: mark + inner + mark },
85:         range: range.empty
86:           ? EditorSelection.cursor(range.from + n)
87:           : EditorSelection.range(range.from + n, range.to + n),
88:       };
89:     });
90:     dispatch(state.update(tr, { userEvent: "input", scrollIntoView: true }));
91:     return true;
92:   };
93: }
94: 
95: export const toggleBold = toggleInline(BOLD);
96: export const toggleItalic = toggleInline(ITALIC);
97: export const toggleInlineCode = toggleInline(CODE);
98: /** GFM `~~strike~~`; no chord, it only exists on the floating panel (5.2). */
99: export const toggleStrike = toggleInline(STRIKE);

101: /** `[selection](  )` with the caret where the url goes. */
102: export const insertLink: StateCommand = ({ state, dispatch }) => {
103:   const tr = state.changeByRange((range) => {
104:     const text = state.sliceDoc(range.from, range.to);
105:     const insert = `[${text}]()`;
106:     return {
107:       changes: { from: range.from, to: range.to, insert },
108:       range: EditorSelection.cursor(
109:         range.empty ? range.from + 1 : range.from + insert.length - 1,
110:       ),
111:     };
112:   });
113:   dispatch(state.update(tr, { userEvent: "input", scrollIntoView: true }));
114:   return true;
115: };
116: 
117: /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ line */
118: 
119: const FENCE = /^\s*(```|~~~)/;
120: 
121: /** Wraps the selected lines in a fence, or unwraps one it is already in. */
122: export const toggleCodeBlock: StateCommand = ({ state, dispatch }) => {
123:   const lines = selectedLines(state);
124:   const first = lines[0];
125:   const last = lines[lines.length - 1];
126:   if (!first || !last) return false;
127: 
128:   const changes: ChangeSpec[] =
129:     lines.length >= 3 && FENCE.test(first.text) && FENCE.test(last.text)
130:       ? [
131:           { from: first.from, to: Math.min(first.to + 1, state.doc.length) },
132:           { from: Math.max(0, last.from - 1), to: last.to },
133:         ]
134:       : [
135:           { from: first.from, insert: "```" + state.lineBreak },
136:           { from: last.to, insert: state.lineBreak + "```" },
137:         ];
138:   dispatch(state.update({ changes, userEvent: "input", scrollIntoView: true }));
139:   return true;
140: };
141: 
142: /** `> ` on every selected line, or off every selected line. */
143: export const toggleQuote: StateCommand = ({ state, dispatch }) => {
144:   const lines = selectedLines(state);
145:   if (lines.length === 0) return false;
146:   const quoted = lines.every((l) => /^\s*>/.test(l.text) || l.text.trim() === "");
147:   const changes: ChangeSpec[] = [];
148:   for (const line of lines) {
149:     if (quoted) {
150:       const m = /^(\s*)> ?/.exec(line.text);
151:       if (m) changes.push({ from: line.from + (m[1]?.length ?? 0), to: line.from + m[0].length });
152:     } else {
153:       const indent = /^\s*/.exec(line.text)?.[0].length ?? 0;
154:       changes.push({ from: line.from + indent, insert: "> " });
155:     }
156:   }
157:   if (changes.length === 0) return false;
158:   dispatch(state.update({ changes, userEvent: "input", scrollIntoView: true }));
159:   return true;
160: };
161: 
162: export type ListKind = "none" | "bullet" | "ordered" | "task";
163: 
164: const TASK = /^(\s*)[-*+] \[[ xX]\] /;
165: const BULLET = /^(\s*)[-*+] /;
166: const ORDERED = /^(\s*)\d+[.)] /;
167: 
168: export function listKind(text: string): ListKind {
169:   if (TASK.test(text)) return "task";
170:   if (BULLET.test(text)) return "bullet";
171:   if (ORDERED.test(text)) return "ordered";
172:   return "none";
173: }
174: 
175: const NEXT: Record<ListKind, ListKind> = {
176:   none: "bullet",
177:   bullet: "ordered",
178:   ordered: "task",
179:   task: "none",
180: };
181: 
182: /** Marker length of whatever list markup the line already carries. */
183: function markerLength(text: string): number {
184:   const m = TASK.exec(text) ?? BULLET.exec(text) ?? ORDERED.exec(text);
185:   return m ? m[0].length - (m[1]?.length ?? 0) : 0;
186: }
187: 
188: /** `-` -> `1.` -> `- [ ]` -> nothing (spec 5.2). */
189: export const cycleList: StateCommand = ({ state, dispatch }) => {
190:   const lines = selectedLines(state).filter((l) => l.text.trim() !== "");
191:   const first = lines[0];
192:   if (!first) return false;
193: 
194:   const next = NEXT[listKind(first.text)];
195:   const changes: ChangeSpec[] = [];
196:   let number = 1;
197:   for (const line of lines) {
198:     const indent = /^\s*/.exec(line.text)?.[0].length ?? 0;
199:     const from = line.from + indent;
200:     const to = from + markerLength(line.text);
201:     const marker =
202:       next === "bullet" ? "- " : next === "ordered" ? `${number++}. ` : next === "task" ? "- [ ] " : "";
203:     changes.push({ from, to, insert: marker });
204:   }
205:   if (changes.length === 0) return false;
206:   dispatch(state.update({ changes, userEvent: "input", scrollIntoView: true }));
207:   return true;
208: };
209: 
210: /** Ctrl+Enter: tick or untick, adding the checkbox when there is none. */
211: export const toggleCheckbox: StateCommand = ({ state, dispatch }) => {
212:   const lines = selectedLines(state);
213:   if (lines.length === 0) return false;
214:   const changes: ChangeSpec[] = [];
215:   for (const line of lines) {
216:     const task = /^(\s*[-*+] )\[([ xX])\] /.exec(line.text);
217:     if (task) {
218:       const at = line.from + (task[1]?.length ?? 0) + 1;
219:       changes.push({ from: at, to: at + 1, insert: task[2] === " " ? "x" : " " });
220:       continue;
221:     }
222:     const bullet = BULLET.exec(line.text);
223:     if (bullet) {
224:       changes.push({ from: line.from + bullet[0].length, insert: "[ ] " });
225:       continue;
226:     }
227:     const indent = /^\s*/.exec(line.text)?.[0].length ?? 0;
228:     changes.push({ from: line.from + indent, insert: "- [ ] " });
229:   }
230:   if (changes.length === 0) return false;
231:   dispatch(state.update({ changes, userEvent: "input", scrollIntoView: true }));
232:   return true;
233: };
234: 
235: /** `#` * level on every selected line; the same level again takes it off. */
236: export function toggleHeading(level: number): StateCommand {
237:   const prefix = "#".repeat(level) + " ";
238:   return ({ state, dispatch }) => {
239:     const lines = selectedLines(state);
240:     if (lines.length === 0) return false;
241:     const changes: ChangeSpec[] = [];
242:     for (const line of lines) {
243:       const m = /^(#{1,6}) +/.exec(line.text);
244:       const to = line.from + (m ? m[0].length : 0);
245:       if (m && m[1]?.length === level) changes.push({ from: line.from, to });
246:       else changes.push({ from: line.from, to, insert: prefix });
247:     }
248:     if (changes.length === 0) return false;
249:     dispatch(state.update({ changes, userEvent: "input", scrollIntoView: true }));
250:     return true;
251:   };
252: }
253: 
254: /** `heading  off` in the menu (spec 4): any `#` prefix comes off. */
255: export const clearHeading: StateCommand = ({ state, dispatch }) => {
256:   const changes: ChangeSpec[] = [];
257:   for (const line of selectedLines(state)) {
258:     const m = /^(#{1,6}) +/.exec(line.text);
259:     if (m) changes.push({ from: line.from, to: line.from + m[0].length });
260:   }
261:   if (changes.length === 0) return false;
262:   dispatch(state.update({ changes, userEvent: "input", scrollIntoView: true }));
263:   return true;
264: };
265: 
266: /* ------------------------------------------------------------------- tab */
267: 
268: /**
269:  * Tab nests the item when the caret sits in the markup at the start of a
270:  * line, and inserts one indent unit anywhere else (spec 5.2).
271:  */
272: export const tabIndent: Command = (view) => {
273:   const { state } = view;
274:   if (state.selection.ranges.some((r) => !r.empty)) return indentMore(view);
275: 
276:   const head = state.selection.main.head;
277:   const line = state.doc.lineAt(head);
278:   const before = line.text.slice(0, head - line.from);
279:   if (/^\s*$/.test(before) || /^\s*([-*+]|\d+[.)])( \[[ xX]\])?\s*$/.test(before)) {
280:     return indentMore(view);
281:   }
282: 
283:   const unit = state.facet(indentUnit);
284:   const tr = state.changeByRange((range) => ({
285:     changes: { from: range.from, insert: unit },
286:     range: EditorSelection.cursor(range.from + unit.length),
287:   }));
288:   view.dispatch(state.update(tr, { userEvent: "input", scrollIntoView: true }));
289:   return true;
290: };
291: 
292: /* ---------------------------------------------------------------- indent */
293: 
294: /** Indent width of the file itself, which wins over settings (spec 10). */
295: export function detectIndent(text: string, fallback: string): string {
296:   const lines = text.split(/\r\n|\r|\n/, 400);
297:   for (const line of lines) {
298:     if (/^\t/.test(line)) return "\t";
299:     const m = /^( +)\S/.exec(line);
300:     if (m) {
301:       const width = m[1]?.length ?? 0;
302:       if (width >= 2 && width <= 8) return " ".repeat(width);
303:     }
304:   }
305:   return fallback;
306: }

FILE src/editor/sync.ts
1: // Editor -> store. Nothing is written from inside a CodeMirror update:
2: // measured on a 1 MB file, a store write on that path cost ~30 ms per
3: // keystroke and 2 ms off it. So the caret rides one frame behind and the text
4: // goes back when the browser is idle (the status bar counts words over it).
5: 
6: import { EditorView } from "@codemirror/view";
7: import type { EditorState } from "@codemirror/state";
8: import type { Doc } from "../app/store";
9: import { useStore } from "../app/store";
10: import { isDirty } from "./buffers";
11: 
12: export function caretOf(state: EditorState): { line: number; col: number } {
13:   const head = state.selection.main.head;
14:   const line = state.doc.lineAt(head);
15:   return { line: line.number, col: head - line.from + 1 };
16: }
17: 
18: let idle: number | null = null;
19: let frame: number | null = null;
20: 
21: function cancelIdle(): void {
22:   if (idle === null) return;
23:   if (typeof window.cancelIdleCallback === "function") window.cancelIdleCallback(idle);
24:   else window.clearTimeout(idle);
25:   idle = null;
26: }
27: 
28: function cancelFrame(): void {
29:   if (frame === null) return;
30:   cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
31:   frame = null;
32: }
33: 
34: /**
35:  * Writes text and caret into the store now - on idle, on unmount and on a
36:  * document switch. It takes the caret too, because it cancels the frame that
37:  * would otherwise have carried it.
38:  */
39: export function flushText(view: EditorView, id: string): void {
40:   cancelIdle();
41:   cancelFrame();
42:   const store = useStore.getState();
43:   const current = store.docs.find((d) => d.id === id);
44:   if (!current) return;
45:   const text = view.state.doc.toString();
46:   const caret = caretOf(view.state);
47:   const patch: Partial<Omit<Doc, "id">> = {};
48:   if (current.text !== text) patch.text = text;
49:   // The authority on `dirty`: the frame below only ever guesses `true`, and
50:   // an undo or a reload from disk has to be able to take it back.
51:   const dirty = isDirty(id, text);
52:   if (current.dirty !== dirty) patch.dirty = dirty;
53:   const previous = current.caret;
54:   if (!previous || previous.line !== caret.line || previous.col !== caret.col) {
55:     patch.caret = caret;
56:   }
57:   if (Object.keys(patch).length > 0) store.updateDoc(id, patch);
58: }
59: 
60: function scheduleText(view: EditorView, id: string): void {
61:   if (idle !== null) return;
62:   const run = () => {
63:     idle = null;
64:     flushText(view, id);
65:   };
66:   idle =
67:     typeof window.requestIdleCallback === "function"
68:       ? window.requestIdleCallback(run, { timeout: 500 })
69:       : window.setTimeout(run, 200);
70: }
71: 
72: /** Caret, plus the dirty dot - it shouldn't wait for the text flush. */
73: function pushCaret(view: EditorView, id: string, dirty: boolean): void {
74:   const store = useStore.getState();
75:   const current = store.docs.find((d) => d.id === id);
76:   if (!current) return;
77:   const caret = caretOf(view.state);
78:   const previous = current.caret;
79:   const patch: Partial<Omit<Doc, "id">> = {};
80:   if (!previous || previous.line !== caret.line || previous.col !== caret.col) {
81:     patch.caret = caret;
82:   }
83:   if (dirty && !current.dirty) patch.dirty = true;
84:   if (Object.keys(patch).length > 0) store.updateDoc(id, patch);
85: }
86: 
87: /** On mount and on document switch the status bar should not lag a frame. */
88: export function syncCaret(view: EditorView, id: string): void {
89:   cancelFrame();
90:   pushCaret(view, id, false);
91: }
92: 
93: function scheduleCaret(view: EditorView, id: string, dirty: boolean): void {
94:   if (frame !== null) return;
95:   frame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
96:     frame = null;
97:     pushCaret(view, id, dirty);
98:   });
99: }
100: 
101: /** The editor always shows the active document, so that is the id to patch. */
102: export const storeSync = EditorView.updateListener.of((update) => {
103:   if (!update.docChanged && !update.selectionSet) return;
104:   const id = useStore.getState().activeId;
105:   if (id === null) return;
106:   if (update.docChanged) scheduleText(update.view, id);
107:   scheduleCaret(update.view, id, update.docChanged);
108: });
FILE src/editor/buffers.ts
1: // One EditorState per document, kept outside React so that leaving edit and
2: // coming back keeps history, selection and scroll (spec 5.0).
3: 
4: import { EditorState } from "@codemirror/state";
5: import { normalizeEol } from "../app/eol";
6: import type { Doc } from "../app/store";
7: import { useStore } from "../app/store";
8: import type { Settings } from "../app/settings";
9: import { editorExtensions } from "./setup";
10: 
11: export interface Buffer {
12:   state: EditorState;
13:   /**
14:    * The text on disk as CodeMirror holds it - line endings normalized to
15:    * `\n`. `dirty` is a comparison against this, so both sides must be
16:    * normalized or a CRLF file would never look clean again. Saving writes
17:    * the file back in its own dominant style (8).
18:    */
19:   savedText: string;
20:   scrollTop: number;
21: }
22: 
23: const buffers = new Map<string, Buffer>();
24: 
25: /** The buffer for a document, created from `doc.text` on first use. */
26: export function buffer(doc: Doc, settings: Settings): Buffer {
27:   const existing = buffers.get(doc.id);
28:   if (existing) return existing;
29:   const state = EditorState.create({
30:     doc: doc.text,
31:     extensions: editorExtensions(doc, settings),
32:   });
33:   // Not the text we just loaded: a restored draft is already unsaved work,
34:   // and its clean text is whatever the file on disk holds (spec 8).
35:   const created: Buffer = {
36:     state,
37:     savedText: normalizeEol(doc.savedText),
38:     scrollTop: 0,
39:   };
40:   buffers.set(doc.id, created);
41:   return created;
42: }
43: 
44: export function keepBuffer(id: string, state: EditorState, scrollTop: number): void {
45:   const existing = buffers.get(id);
46:   if (existing) {
47:     existing.state = state;
48:     existing.scrollTop = scrollTop;
49:   }
50: }
51: 
52: /** Called after a successful save, with the text that reached the disk. */
53: export function markSaved(id: string, text: string): void {
54:   const existing = buffers.get(id);
55:   if (existing) existing.savedText = normalizeEol(text);
56: }
57: 
58: /**
59:  * The one dirty test. A document that has never been in edit has no buffer,
60:  * so the document's own copy of the disk text answers for it.
61:  */
62: export function isDirty(id: string, text: string): boolean {
63:   const existing = buffers.get(id);
64:   if (existing) return existing.savedText !== normalizeEol(text);
65:   const doc = useStore.getState().docs.find((d) => d.id === id);
66:   return doc ? normalizeEol(doc.savedText) !== normalizeEol(text) : false;
67: }
68: 
69: /** An external reload, applied as one change so undo has a single step. */
70: export function setBufferText(id: string, text: string): void {
71:   const existing = buffers.get(id);
72:   if (!existing) return;
73:   existing.state = existing.state.update({
74:     changes: { from: 0, to: existing.state.doc.length, insert: text },
75:   }).state;
76: }
77: 
78: /** Save As keeps the buffer - history and all - under the new file's id. */
79: export function moveBuffer(from: string, to: string): void {
80:   const existing = buffers.get(from);
81:   if (!existing || from === to) return;
82:   buffers.delete(from);
83:   buffers.set(to, existing);
84: }
85: 
86: export function dropBuffer(id: string): void {
87:   buffers.delete(id);
88: }
89: 
90: // A closed document must not leave its buffer behind: opening the file again
91: // has to read it from disk, not from a state we kept.
92: useStore.subscribe((state, previous) => {
93:   if (state.docs.length >= previous.docs.length) return;
94:   for (const id of buffers.keys()) {
95:     if (!state.docs.some((d) => d.id === id)) buffers.delete(id);
96:   }
97: });
98: 
99: /** Tests only. */
100: export function clearBuffers(): void {
101:   buffers.clear();
102: }
FILE src/editor/EditView.tsx
1: import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
2: import { EditorView, type Command as EditorCommand } from "@codemirror/view";
3: import type { Doc } from "../app/store";
4: import { useStore } from "../app/store";
5: import { buffer, keepBuffer, markSaved, moveBuffer, setBufferText } from "./buffers";
6: import { headingAt } from "./headings";
7: import { gutterConf, gutterExtension, lineNumbersOn, onLineNumbers } from "./setup";
8: import { flushText, syncCaret } from "./sync";
9: import "../ui/editor.css";
10: 
11: const GOTO_LINE = "plain:goto-line";
12: const GOTO_HEADING = "plain:goto-heading";
13: /** A jump sent just before this view mounts is still ours; an older one is not. */
14: const PENDING_MS = 2000;
15: 
16: let live: EditorView | null = null;
17: let liveId: string | null = null;
18: let pending: { line: number; at: number } | null = null;
19: 
20: /**
21:  * Puts whatever the editor holds into the store synchronously. Wave 4 calls
22:  * this before saving, so the snapshot never waits for the idle callback.
23:  */
24: export function flushActiveEditor(): void {
25:   if (live && liveId) flushText(live, liveId);
26: }
27: 
28: /**
29:  * Runs a CodeMirror command against whichever editor is on screen. The menu
30:  * bar needs this: undo, bold and friends are editor commands, and the menu
31:  * has no view of its own (spec 4). Nothing mounted - nothing happens.
32:  */
33: export function runEditorCommand(command: EditorCommand): boolean {
34:   if (!live) return false;
35:   // The menu took the focus away; the command works on the selection anyway,
36:   // but the caret has to come back or the next keystroke goes nowhere.
37:   live.focus();
38:   return command(live);
39: }
40: 
41: /**
42:  * Replaces the whole document in one transaction, so a silent reload from
43:  * disk is a single undo step (spec 8). The new text becomes the clean one.
44:  */
45: export function replaceText(id: string, text: string): void {
46:   if (live && liveId === id) {
47:     live.dispatch({ changes: { from: 0, to: live.state.doc.length, insert: text } });
48:     keepBuffer(id, live.state, live.scrollDOM.scrollTop);
49:     markSaved(id, text);
50:     // The dispatch queued a sync that would report the document as edited;
51:     // running it now, after the new text is the clean one, settles it.
52:     flushText(live, id);
53:     return;
54:   }
55:   setBufferText(id, text);
56:   markSaved(id, text);
57: }
58: 
59: /**
60:  * Save As: the buffer follows the document to its new id. Whatever the live
61:  * editor holds is stored first, or the move would carry a stale state.
62:  */
63: export function renameBuffer(from: string, to: string): void {
64:   if (live && liveId === from) {
65:     keepBuffer(from, live.state, live.scrollDOM.scrollTop);
66:     liveId = to;
67:   }
68:   moveBuffer(from, to);
69: }
70: 
71: function jump(view: EditorView, line: number): void {
72:   const target = view.state.doc.line(Math.min(Math.max(1, line), view.state.doc.lines));
73:   // Focus first: focusing after the transaction makes the browser scroll to
74:   // where the selection used to be, undoing the jump.
75:   view.focus();
76:   view.dispatch({
77:     selection: { anchor: target.from },
78:     effects: EditorView.scrollIntoView(target.from, { y: "start" }),
79:   });
80: }
81: 
82: // The outline and read -> edit send this; it can arrive before the view is
83: // mounted, so it waits here until it is. The listener lives on the module so
84: // it is there before the first mount; the window slot keeps a hot reload from
85: // leaving the previous module's listener (and its dead view) behind.
86: const LISTENER_SLOT = "__plainGotoLine";
87: 
88: if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
89:   const slot = window as unknown as Record<string, EventListener | undefined>;
90:   const previous = slot[LISTENER_SLOT];
91:   if (previous) window.removeEventListener(GOTO_LINE, previous);
92:   const onGotoLine: EventListener = (event) => {
93:     const line = (event as CustomEvent<number>).detail;
94:     if (typeof line !== "number") return;
95:     if (live) jump(live, line);
96:     else pending = { line, at: Date.now() };
97:   };
98:   slot[LISTENER_SLOT] = onGotoLine;
99:   window.addEventListener(GOTO_LINE, onGotoLine);
100: }
101: 
102: function takePending(view: EditorView): void {
103:   if (pending && Date.now() - pending.at < PENDING_MS) jump(view, pending.line);
104:   pending = null;
105: }
106: 
107: /** Leaving edit: tell read which heading the top of the screen was under. */
108: function announceHeading(view: EditorView, id: string): void {
109:   const store = useStore.getState();
110:   const doc = store.docs.find((d) => d.id === id);
111:   if (store.activeId !== id || doc?.mode !== "read") return;
112:   let line = 1;
113:   try {
114:     const top = view.scrollDOM.getBoundingClientRect().top - view.documentTop;
115:     line = view.state.doc.lineAt(view.lineBlockAtHeight(top).from).number;
116:   } catch {
117:     return;
118:   }
119:   const heading = headingAt(view.state.doc.toString(), line);
120:   if (!heading) return;
121:   // After the current commit, so the read view is already listening.
122:   window.setTimeout(() => {
123:     window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(GOTO_HEADING, { detail: heading.id }));
124:   }, 0);
125: }
126: 
127: export function EditView({ doc }: { doc: Doc }) {
128:   const host = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
129:   const view = useRef<EditorView | null>(null);
130:   const shown = useRef(doc.id);
131:   const [numbers, setNumbers] = useState(lineNumbersOn);
132: 
133:   useEffect(() => onLineNumbers(() => setNumbers(lineNumbersOn())), []);
134: 
135:   useEffect(() => {
136:     const parent = host.current;
137:     if (!parent) return;
138:     const { settings } = useStore.getState();
139:     const opened = buffer(doc, settings);
140:     const created = new EditorView({ state: opened.state, parent });
141:     view.current = created;
142:     live = created;
143:     liveId = doc.id;
144:     shown.current = doc.id;
145:     syncCaret(created, doc.id);
146:     // Focus first: focusing scrolls the caret into view, so anything we do
147:     // to the scroll position has to come after it.
148:     created.focus();
149:     created.scrollDOM.scrollTop = opened.scrollTop;
150:     takePending(created);
151: 
152:     return () => {
153:       const id = shown.current;
154:       flushText(created, id);
155:       keepBuffer(id, created.state, created.scrollDOM.scrollTop);
156:       announceHeading(created, id);
157:       live = null;
158:       liveId = null;
159:       view.current = null;
160:       created.destroy();
161:     };
162:     // Mount only: switching documents is handled below, on the same view.
163:   }, []);
164: 
165:   // Another document became active while we stay in edit.
166:   useEffect(() => {
167:     const current = view.current;
168:     if (!current || shown.current === doc.id) return;
169:     const previous = shown.current;
170:     flushText(current, previous);
171:     keepBuffer(previous, current.state, current.scrollDOM.scrollTop);
172: 
173:     const next = buffer(doc, useStore.getState().settings);
174:     current.setState(next.state);
175:     current.dispatch({ effects: gutterConf.reconfigure(gutterExtension(lineNumbersOn())) });
176:     shown.current = doc.id;
177:     liveId = doc.id;
178:     syncCaret(current, doc.id);
179:     current.focus();
180:     current.scrollDOM.scrollTop = next.scrollTop;
181:     takePending(current);
182:   }, [doc.id]);
183: 
184:   useEffect(() => {
185:     view.current?.dispatch({ effects: gutterConf.reconfigure(gutterExtension(numbers)) });
186:   }, [numbers]);
187: 
188:   return <div className={"edit" + (numbers ? "" : " edit-no-gutter")} ref={host} />;
189: }

1: // One place that assembles the CodeMirror extensions. The only compartment
2: // is the line-number gutter, which Ctrl+Shift+9 flips while the app runs.

34: } from "@codemirror/view";

46: export const gutterConf = new Compartment();
47: 
48: /* ------------------------------------------------- soft wrap continuation */
49: 
50: // Leading blockquote/list markup, so a wrapped line lines up with its text.
51: const MARKUP = /^(?:[ \t]*(?:>[ \t]?|[-*+][ \t]+|\d+[.)][ \t]+))*[ \t]*/;
52: 
53: /** Last line of a leading `---` block; 0 when the file has no front matter. */
54: function frontMatterEnd(state: EditorState): number {
55:   if (state.doc.lines < 2 || !/^---\s*$/.test(state.doc.line(1).text)) return 0;
56:   const limit = Math.min(state.doc.lines, 200);
57:   for (let n = 2; n <= limit; n += 1) {
58:     if (/^(---|\.\.\.)\s*$/.test(state.doc.line(n).text)) return n;
59:   }
60:   return 0;
61: }
62: 
63: /** Fenced code, so the whole block gets one background like read does. */
64: function inCode(state: EditorState, pos: number): boolean {
65:   for (let node: SyntaxNode | null = syntaxTree(state).resolveInner(pos, 1); node; node = node.parent) {
66:     if (node.name === "FencedCode" || node.name === "CodeBlock") return true;
67:   }
68:   return false;
69: }
70: 
71: function lineDecorations(view: EditorView): DecorationSet {
72:   const builder = new RangeSetBuilder<Decoration>();
73:   const { state } = view;
74:   const frontMatter = frontMatterEnd(state);
75:   for (const { from, to } of view.visibleRanges) {
76:     for (let pos = from; pos <= to; ) {
77:       const line = state.doc.lineAt(pos);
78:       const markup = MARKUP.exec(line.text)?.[0] ?? "";
79:       const width = markup ? Math.min(countColumn(markup, state.tabSize), 24) : 0;
80:       const cls =
81:         line.number <= frontMatter
82:           ? "cm-frontmatter"
83:           : inCode(state, line.from)
84:             ? "cm-code"
85:             : "";
86:       if (width > 0 || cls) {
87:         builder.add(
88:           line.from,
89:           line.from,
90:           Decoration.line({
91:             class: cls || undefined,
92:             attributes:
93:               width > 0
94:                 ? { style: `text-indent:-${width}ch;padding-left:${width}ch` }
95:                 : undefined,
96:           }),
97:         );
98:       }
99:       pos = line.to + 1;
100:     }
101:   }
102:   return builder.finish();
103: }
104: 
105: const lineLook = ViewPlugin.fromClass(
106:   class {
107:     decorations: DecorationSet;
108:     constructor(view: EditorView) {
109:       this.decorations = lineDecorations(view);
110:     }
111:     update(update: ViewUpdate) {
112:       if (update.docChanged || update.viewportChanged) {
113:         this.decorations = lineDecorations(update.view);
114:       }
115:     }
116:   },
117:   { decorations: (v) => v.decorations },
118: );
119: 
120: /* ----------------------------------------------------- line numbers (12) */
121: 
122: // Ctrl+Shift+9 flips them for the session; settings.json is the starting point.
123: let numbersOverride: boolean | null = null;
124: const numbersListeners = new Set<() => void>();
125: 
126: export function lineNumbersOn(): boolean {
127:   return numbersOverride ?? useStore.getState().settings.edit.lineNumbers;
128: }
129: 
130: export function toggleLineNumbers(): void {
131:   numbersOverride = !lineNumbersOn();
132:   for (const listener of numbersListeners) listener();
133: }
134: 
135: export function onLineNumbers(listener: () => void): () => void {
136:   numbersListeners.add(listener);
137:   return () => numbersListeners.delete(listener);
138: }
139: 
140: /**
141:  * The settings screen moved the base, so the session override is stale - the
142:  * setting is what the user just looked at (spec 10).
143:  */
144: export function syncLineNumbers(): void {
145:   numbersOverride = null;
146:   for (const listener of numbersListeners) listener();
147: }
148: 
149: export function gutterExtension(on: boolean): Extension {
150:   return on ? lineNumbers() : [];
151: }
152: 
153: /* ------------------------------------------------------------- assembling */
154: 
155: const reducedMotion =
156:   typeof window !== "undefined" &&
157:   window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;
158: 
159: // Enter continues the list, the quote or the checkbox; on an empty item it
160: // takes a level off instead of loosening the list (spec 5.2).
161: const markupKeymap = Prec.high(
162:   keymap.of([
163:     { key: "Enter", run: insertNewlineContinueMarkupCommand({ nonTightLists: false }) },
164:     { key: "Backspace", run: deleteMarkupBackward },
165:   ]),
166: );

168: /** No language on big files: no parse, no highlighting (spec 8). */
169: function languageExtension(large: boolean): Extension {
170:   if (large) return [];
171:   return [
172:     markdown({ base: markdownLanguage, codeLanguages: languages, addKeymap: false }),
173:     markupKeymap,
174:   ];
175: }
176: 
177: export function editorExtensions(doc: Doc, settings: Settings): Extension {
178:   const unit = settings.edit.indentUnit === "tab" ? "\t" : " ".repeat(settings.edit.indentUnit);
179:   return [
180:     editorTheme,
181:     syntaxHighlighting(highlightStyle),
182:     history(),
183:     drawSelection({ cursorBlinkRate: reducedMotion ? 0 : 1100 }),
184:     dropCursor(),
185:     EditorState.allowMultipleSelections.of(true),
186:     EditorView.clickAddsSelectionRange.of((event) => event.altKey),
187:     EditorState.readOnly.of(doc.readOnly),
188:     // Microcopy is lowercase latin (spec 1.4); this is CodeMirror's own.
189:     EditorState.phrases.of({ "Go to line": "go to line", go: "go", close: "?" }),
190:     EditorView.lineWrapping,
191:     lineLook,
192:     codeFolding(),
193:     closeBrackets(),
194:     search({ top: true, createPanel: findPanel }),
195:     gutterConf.of(gutterExtension(lineNumbersOn())),
196:     indentUnit.of(detectIndent(doc.text, unit)),
197:     languageExtension(doc.large),
198:     storeSync,
199:     remoteCommands,
200:     formatToolbar,
201:     editorKeymap(),
202:     keymap.of([...closeBracketsKeymap, ...historyKeymap, ...searchKeymap, ...defaultKeymap]),
203:   ];
204: }
