FILE src/read/pipeline.ts
1: // The one markdown pipeline (spec 5.1, 11, 14).
2: //
3: //   remark-parse + gfm + math + front matter + wikilinks + callouts + ==mark==
4: //   -> remark-rehype (dangerous html) -> rehype-raw -> rehype-sanitize
5: //   -> rehype-slug -> KaTeX -> code frames -> HTML string
6: //
7: // Everything here is synchronous: read is built on entering read, once.
8: // Shiki and Mermaid are async and live in the DOM pass (highlight.ts,
9: // mermaid.ts), because they cannot run inside `processSync`.
10: 
11: import katex from "katex";
12: import rehypeRaw from "rehype-raw";
13: import rehypeSanitize, { defaultSchema } from "rehype-sanitize";
14: import rehypeSlug from "rehype-slug";
15: import rehypeStringify from "rehype-stringify";
16: import remarkFrontmatter from "remark-frontmatter";
17: import remarkGfm from "remark-gfm";
18: import remarkMarkers from "remark-flexible-markers";
19: import remarkMath from "remark-math";
20: import remarkParse from "remark-parse";
21: import remarkRehype from "remark-rehype";
22: import { unified, type Plugin } from "unified";
23: import { visit, SKIP } from "unist-util-visit";
24: import { parse as parseYaml } from "yaml";
25: import type { Element, ElementContent, Root as HastRoot, Properties } from "hast";
26: import type { Root as MdastRoot } from "mdast";
27: import type { Options as SanitizeSchema } from "rehype-sanitize";
28: import type { Heading } from "../app/store";
29: import { remarkCallouts } from "./callout";
30: import { remarkWikiLink } from "./wikilink";
31: import { countWords } from "./words";
32: 
33: export interface RenderResult {
34:   html: string;
35:   headings: Heading[];
36:   words: number;
37:   frontmatter: Record<string, unknown> | null;
38: }
39: 
40: /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ utils */
41: 
42: function classList(node: Element): string[] {
43:   const value: unknown = node.properties?.["className"];
44:   if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(String);
45:   return typeof value === "string" ? value.split(/\s+/) : [];
46: }
47: 
48: /** Plain text of a hast subtree; `raw` nodes (KaTeX output) count as text. */
49: function textOf(node: unknown): string {
50:   const it = node as { type?: string; value?: unknown; children?: unknown };
51:   if (it.type === "comment" || it.type === "doctype") return "";
52:   if (typeof it.value === "string") return it.value;
53:   return Array.isArray(it.children) ? it.children.map(textOf).join("") : "";
54: }
55: 
56: function element(
57:   tagName: string,
58:   properties: Properties,
59:   children: ElementContent[],
60: ): Element {
61:   return { type: "element", tagName, properties, children };
62: }
63: 
64: /* ----------------------------------------------------------------- schema */
65: 
66: const base = defaultSchema;
67: const baseAttrs = base.attributes ?? {};
68: 
69: /**
70:  * GitHub schema plus exactly what our own constructs need. `clobber` is off:
71:  * ids come from our own markdown (rehype-slug, GFM footnotes), and prefixing
72:  * them would break every footnote link.
73:  */
74: const schema: SanitizeSchema = {
75:   ...base,
76:   clobber: [],
77:   strip: ["script", "style", "iframe", "object", "embed", "form", "textarea", "title"],
78:   tagNames: [...(base.tagNames ?? []), "mark"],
79:   attributes: {
80:     ...baseAttrs,
81:     // One entry per property name: hast-util-sanitize stops at the first
82:     // definition it finds, so the GitHub `className` rule has to be merged
83:     // rather than appended to.
84:     a: [
85:       "ariaDescribedBy",
86:       "ariaLabel",
87:       "ariaLabelledBy",
88:       "dataFootnoteBackref",
89:       "dataFootnoteRef",
90:       ["className", "data-footnote-backref", "wikilink"],
91:       "href",
92:       "dataWiki",
93:       "dataWikiHash",
94:     ],
95:     code: [["className", /^language-./, "math-inline", "math-display"], "dataLang"],
96:     div: [
97:       ...(baseAttrs["div"] ?? []),
98:       ["className", "callout", "callout-title", "footnotes-label"],
99:       "dataCallout",
100:     ],
101:     span: [["className", "callout-type", "callout-heading"]],
102:     // `src` never survives the pipeline: rehypeDeferImages moves it out of
103:     // the way so nothing is fetched before the reader asks for it (spec 1.3).
104:     img: [...(baseAttrs["img"] ?? []), "dataSrc"],
105:   },
106: };
107: 
108: /* ---------------------------------------------------------------- plugins */
109: 
110: /**
111:  * Currency, not maths (spec 11): a single-dollar span only counts when there
112:  * is no space just inside either delimiter and no digit right after the
113:  * closing one. `$5 � $10` fails both tests and goes back to being text.
114:  */
115: const remarkMathGuard: Plugin<[], MdastRoot> = () => (tree, file) => {
116:   const source = String(file);
117: 
118:   visit(tree, "inlineMath", (node, index, parent) => {
119:     if (!parent || index === undefined) return;
120:     const start = node.position?.start.offset;
121:     const end = node.position?.end.offset;
122:     if (start === undefined || end === undefined) return;
123: 
124:     const raw = source.slice(start, end);
125:     let fence = 0;
126:     while (raw[fence] === "$") fence += 1;
127:     if (fence !== 1) return; // `$$:$$` inline is never ambiguous
128: 
129:     const inner = raw.slice(1, -1);
130:     const after = source.slice(end, end + 1);
131:     const ok = inner !== "" && !/^\s/.test(inner) && !/\s$/.test(inner) && !/[0-9]/.test(after);
132:     if (ok) return;
133: 
134:     parent.children[index] = { type: "text", value: raw };
135:     return SKIP;
136:   });
137: };
138: 
139: interface FrontmatterBox {
140:   value: Record<string, unknown> | null;
141: }
142: 
143: const remarkFrontmatterValue: Plugin<[FrontmatterBox], MdastRoot> = (box) => (tree) => {
144:   const node = tree.children.find((child) => child.type === "yaml");
145:   if (!node || node.type !== "yaml") return;
146:   try {
147:     const parsed: unknown = parseYaml(node.value);
148:     box.value =
149:       parsed && typeof parsed === "object" && !Array.isArray(parsed)
150:         ? (parsed as Record<string, unknown>)
151:         : null;
152:   } catch {
153:     box.value = null;
154:   }
155: };
156: 
157: /** Parks every image source in `data-src`; the DOM pass decides what loads. */
158: const rehypeDeferImages: Plugin<[], HastRoot> = () => (tree) => {
159:   visit(tree, "element", (node) => {
160:     if (node.tagName !== "img" || !node.properties) return;
161:     const src = node.properties["src"];
162:     if (typeof src === "string") node.properties["dataSrc"] = src;
163:     delete node.properties["src"];
164:   });
165: };
166: 
167: const rehypeHeadings: Plugin<[Heading[]], HastRoot> = (out) => (tree) => {
168:   visit(tree, "element", (node) => {
169:     const match = /^h([1-6])$/.exec(node.tagName);
170:     if (!match?.[1]) return;
171:     const id = node.properties?.["id"];
172:     out.push({
173:       level: Number(match[1]),
174:       text: textOf(node).trim(),
175:       id: typeof id === "string" ? id : "",
176:       line: node.position?.start.line ?? 1,
177:     });
178:   });
179: };
180: 
181: function renderMath(value: string, display: boolean): ElementContent {
182:   const html = katex.renderToString(value.trim(), {
183:     displayMode: display,
184:     throwOnError: false,
185:     output: "html",
186:     strict: false,
187:   });
188:   return { type: "raw", value: html } as unknown as ElementContent;
189: }
190: 
191: /**
192:  * Runs after sanitize on purpose: KaTeX leans on inline `style` for its
193:  * spacing, and the GitHub schema strips that attribute.
194:  */
195: const rehypeKatex: Plugin<[], HastRoot> = () => (tree) => {
196:   visit(tree, "element", (node, index, parent) => {
197:     if (!parent || index === undefined) return;
198: 
199:     if (node.tagName === "pre") {
200:       const child = node.children.find((c) => c.type === "element");
201:       if (child?.type !== "element" || !classList(child).includes("math-display")) return;
202:       parent.children[index] = renderMath(textOf(child), true);
203:       return SKIP;
204:     }
205: 
206:     if (node.tagName === "code" && classList(node).includes("math-inline")) {
207:       parent.children[index] = renderMath(textOf(node), false);
208:       return SKIP;
209:     }
210: 
211:     return;
212:   });
213: };
214: 
215: /** Wraps every code block in a frame with a language label and `copy`. */
216: const rehypeCodeFrame: Plugin<[], HastRoot> = () => (tree) => {
217:   visit(tree, "element", (node, index, parent) => {
218:     if (!parent || index === undefined || node.tagName !== "pre") return;
219: 
220:     const code = node.children.find((c) => c.type === "element" && c.tagName === "code");
221:     if (code?.type !== "element") return;
222: 
223:     const lang =
224:       classList(code)
225:         .find((name) => name.startsWith("language-"))
226:         ?.slice("language-".length) ?? "";
227:     const mermaid = lang === "mermaid";
228: 
229:     if (code.properties) code.properties["dataLang"] = lang;
230: 
231:     const bar = element("div", { className: ["code-bar"] }, [
232:       element("span", { className: ["code-lang"] }, [{ type: "text", value: lang || "text" }]),
233:       element("button", { className: ["code-copy"], type: "button" }, [
234:         { type: "text", value: "copy" },
235:       ]),
236:     ]);
237: 
238:     parent.children[index] = element(
239:       "div",
240:       {
241:         className: mermaid ? ["codeblock", "mermaid-block"] : ["codeblock"],
242:         dataLang: lang,
243:       },
244:       [bar, node],
245:     );
246:     return SKIP;
247:   });
248: };
249: 
250: /* ----------------------------------------------------------------- render */
251: 
252: export function render(text: string): RenderResult {
253:   const headings: Heading[] = [];
254:   const frontmatter: FrontmatterBox = { value: null };
255: 
256:   const file = unified()
257:     .use(remarkParse)
258:     .use(remarkFrontmatter, ["yaml"])
259:     .use(remarkGfm)
260:     .use(remarkMath, { singleDollarTextMath: true })
261:     .use(remarkMathGuard)
262:     .use(remarkMarkers, { markerTagName: "mark", markerClassName: () => [] })
263:     .use(remarkWikiLink)
264:     .use(remarkCallouts)
265:     .use(remarkFrontmatterValue, frontmatter)
266:     .use(remarkRehype, {
267:       allowDangerousHtml: true,
268:       footnoteLabel: "footnotes",
269:       footnoteLabelTagName: "div",
270:       footnoteLabelProperties: { className: ["footnotes-label"] },
271:     })
272:     .use(rehypeRaw)
273:     .use(rehypeDeferImages)
274:     .use(rehypeSanitize, schema)
275:     .use(rehypeSlug)
276:     .use(rehypeHeadings, headings)
277:     .use(rehypeKatex)
278:     .use(rehypeCodeFrame)
279:     .use(rehypeStringify, { allowDangerousHtml: true })
280:     .processSync(text);
281: 
282:   return {
283:     html: String(file),
284:     headings,
285:     words: countWords(text),
286:     frontmatter: frontmatter.value,
287:   };
288: }
FILE src/read/assets.ts
1: // Local images ride the Tauri asset protocol, whose scope is handed out one
2: // folder at a time (spec 9): the folder of the open file, the library, and
3: // anything the reader allows from an `outside folder` placeholder.
4: 
5: import { convertFileSrc, invoke } from "@tauri-apps/api/core";
6: import { inTauri } from "../app/env";
7: import { isInside, normalizePath } from "./links";
8: 
9: const granted = new Set<string>();
10: 
11: /** Folders allowed so far this session (lowercased, forward slashes). */
12: export function isAllowedPath(path: string): boolean {
13:   if (!inTauri) return true;
14:   for (const dir of granted) if (isInside(path, dir)) return true;
15:   return false;
16: }
17: 
18: /**
19:  * Widens the asset protocol scope - and, on the Rust side, the file-system
20:  * read scope, so that links into the same folder can be opened.
21:  */
22: export async function allowAssetDir(dir: string | null): Promise<boolean> {
23:   if (!dir) return false;
24:   const path = normalizePath(dir);
25:   const key = path.toLowerCase();
26:   if (granted.has(key)) return true;
27:   if (!inTauri) {
28:     granted.add(key);
29:     return true;
30:   }
31:   try {
32:     await invoke("allow_asset_dir", { path });
33:     granted.add(key);
34:     return true;
35:   } catch {
36:     return false;
37:   }
38: }
39: 
40: export function assetUrl(path: string): string {
41:   return inTauri ? convertFileSrc(path) : path;
42: }
FILE src/read/links.ts
1: // Where a link in the rendered document points (spec 5.1, 9).
2: //
3: // Paths are kept with forward slashes: Windows accepts them everywhere, and
4: // it keeps joining, comparing and `convertFileSrc` free of separator noise.
5: 
6: import { slug } from "github-slugger";
7: 
8: export type LinkTarget =
9:   | { kind: "external"; url: string }
10:   | { kind: "anchor"; id: string }
11:   | { kind: "file"; path: string; hash: string | null }
12:   | { kind: "none" };
13: 
14: const EXTERNAL = /^(?:https?|mailto|tel):/i;
15: const HAS_EXTENSION = /\.[A-Za-z0-9]{1,8}$/;
16: 
17: /** Percent-decoding that survives a stray `%` (spec: `%20`, Cyrillic). */
18: export function decode(value: string): string {
19:   try {
20:     return decodeURIComponent(value);
21:   } catch {
22:     return value;
23:   }
24: }
25: 
26: export function isAbsolutePath(path: string): boolean {
27:   return /^[A-Za-z]:[\\/]/.test(path) || /^[\\/]{2}/.test(path);
28: }
29: 
30: /** Joins `rel` onto `dir`, resolving `.` and `..`. */
31: export function joinPath(dir: string, rel: string): string {
32:   const parts = dir.replace(/\\/g, "/").replace(/\/+$/, "").split("/");
33:   for (const segment of rel.replace(/\\/g, "/").split("/")) {
34:     if (segment === "" || segment === ".") continue;
35:     if (segment === "..") {
36:       if (parts.length > 1) parts.pop();
37:       continue;
38:     }
39:     parts.push(segment);
40:   }
41:   return parts.join("/");
42: }
43: 
44: export function normalizePath(path: string): string {
45:   return path.replace(/\\/g, "/").replace(/\/+$/, "");
46: }
47: 
48: /** Case-insensitive "is this file inside that folder" (Windows). */
49: export function isInside(path: string, dir: string): boolean {
50:   const a = normalizePath(path).toLowerCase();
51:   const b = normalizePath(dir).toLowerCase();
52:   return a === b || a.startsWith(b + "/");
53: }
54: 
55: function fromPath(raw: string, dir: string | null): LinkTarget {
56:   const at = raw.indexOf("#");
57:   const rawPath = at < 0 ? raw : raw.slice(0, at);
58:   const hash = at < 0 ? null : decode(raw.slice(at + 1)) || null;
59:   const path = decode(rawPath);
60: 
61:   if (path === "") return hash ? { kind: "anchor", id: hash } : { kind: "none" };
62:   if (isAbsolutePath(path)) return { kind: "file", path: normalizePath(path), hash };
63:   if (!dir) return { kind: "none" };
64:   return { kind: "file", path: joinPath(dir, path), hash };
65: }
66: 
67: /** `href` of an ordinary markdown link, seen from the folder of `dir`. */
68: export function resolveHref(href: string, dir: string | null): LinkTarget {
69:   const value = href.trim();
70:   if (value === "") return { kind: "none" };
71:   if (EXTERNAL.test(value)) return { kind: "external", url: value };
72:   if (value.startsWith("#")) return { kind: "anchor", id: decode(value.slice(1)) };
73:   return fromPath(value, dir);
74: }
75: 
76: /**
77:  * `[[target#Heading]]` - the target is a file name without an extension most
78:  * of the time, and the heading turns into the same slug rehype-slug produced.
79:  */
80: export function resolveWikiLink(
81:   target: string,
82:   hash: string | null,
83:   dir: string | null,
84: ): LinkTarget {
85:   const raw = decode(target.trim());
86:   if (raw === "") return { kind: "none" };
87:   const withExtension = HAS_EXTENSION.test(raw) ? raw : `${raw}.md`;
88:   const anchor = hash ? slug(hash.trim()) : null;
89: 
90:   if (isAbsolutePath(withExtension)) {
91:     return { kind: "file", path: normalizePath(withExtension), hash: anchor };
92:   }
93:   if (!dir) return { kind: "none" };
94:   return { kind: "file", path: joinPath(dir, withExtension), hash: anchor };
95: }
96: 
97: /** Folder part of a path, with forward slashes. */
98: export function folderOf(path: string): string {
99:   const normal = normalizePath(path);
100:   const at = normal.lastIndexOf("/");
101:   return at > 0 ? normal.slice(0, at) : normal;
102: }
103: 
104: /** `notes/a.md` - what the status bar shows while hovering a link. */
105: export function shortPath(path: string, dir: string | null): string {
106:   if (dir && isInside(path, dir)) {
107:     return normalizePath(path).slice(normalizePath(dir).length + 1);
108:   }
109:   return normalizePath(path);
110: }

35: function formatDate(date: Date): string {

37: }

39: function frontmatterDate(value: unknown): Date | null {
40:   if (value instanceof Date) return Number.isNaN(value.getTime()) ? null : value;
41:   if (typeof value !== "string" && typeof value !== "number") return null;
42:   const date = new Date(value);
43:   return Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? null : date;
44: }

61: async function copy(text: string): Promise<void> {

63:     await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
64:   } catch {
65:     /* nothing to do; copying is a convenience */
66:   }

69: export function ReadView({ doc }: { doc: Doc }) {
70:   const theme = useStore((s) => s.resolvedTheme);
71:   const libraryPath = useStore((s) => s.libraryPath);
72: 
73:   const [nonce, setNonce] = useState(0);
74:   const [forced, setForced] = useState(false);
75:   const [scopeReady, setScopeReady] = useState(false);
76:   const [findOpen, setFindOpen] = useState(false);
77:   const [findFocus, setFindFocus] = useState(0);
78:   const [mtime, setMtime] = useState<Date | null>(null);
79: 
80:   const scroller = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
81:   const body = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
82:   const topLine = useRef(1);
83:   const ticking = useRef(false);
84: 
85:   const docId = doc.id;
86:   const dir = doc.path ? folderOf(doc.path) : null;
87:   const large = doc.text.length > LARGE;
88:   const skip = large && !forced;
89: 
90:   // A document that cannot be built must not take the window down with it.
91:   const built = useMemo(() => {
92:     if (skip) return null;
93:     try {
94:       return { result: render(doc.text), error: null };
95:     } catch (error) {
96:       return { result: null, error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) };
97:     }
98:     // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
99:   }, [doc.text, skip, nonce]);
100: 
101:   const result = built?.result ?? null;
102:   const failure = built?.error ?? null;
103:   const html = result?.html ?? "";
104:   const headings = result?.headings;
105: 
106:   /* ------------------------------------------------------------- outline */
107: 
108:   const lines = useMemo(() => {
109:     const map = new Map<string, number>();
110:     for (const heading of headings ?? []) map.set(heading.id, heading.line);
111:     return map;
112:   }, [headings]);
113: 
114:   useEffect(() => {
115:     if (headings) useStore.getState().updateDoc(docId, { headings });
116:   }, [docId, headings]);
117: 
118:   const trackHeading = useCallback(() => {
119:     const frame = scroller.current;
120:     const content = body.current;
121:     if (!frame || !content) return;
122:     const edge = frame.getBoundingClientRect().top + 8;
123:     let seen: HTMLElement | null = null;
124:     for (const heading of content.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>("h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6")) {
125:       if (heading.getBoundingClientRect().top > edge) break;
126:       seen = heading;
127:     }
128:     // Above the first heading the outline still points at it, not at nothing.
129:     const id = seen?.id ?? content.querySelector("h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6")?.id ?? null;
130:     setActiveHeading(id || null);
131:     topLine.current = (id ? lines.get(id) : undefined) ?? 1;
132:     // Where you were reading is remembered per file, LRU 300 (spec 8).
133:     if (id && doc.path) rememberReading(doc.path, id);
134:   }, [lines, doc.path]);
135: 
136:   /* --------------------------------------------------------------- scope */
137: 
138:   useEffect(() => {
139:     setScopeReady(false);
140:     void Promise.all([allowAssetDir(dir), allowAssetDir(libraryPath)]).then(() =>
141:       setScopeReady(true),
142:     );
143:   }, [dir, libraryPath]);
144: 
145:   /* ---------------------------------------------------------------- meta */
146: 
147:   // Every document asks for itself: `render anyway` is not a session setting.
148:   useEffect(() => {
149:     setForced(false);
150:   }, [docId]);
151: 
152:   useEffect(() => {
153:     setMtime(null);
154:     if (!inTauri || !doc.path) return;
155:     let live = true;
156:     void stat(doc.path)
157:       .then((info) => {
158:         if (live && info.mtime) setMtime(info.mtime);
159:       })
160:       .catch(() => undefined);
161:     return () => {
162:       live = false;
163:     };
164:   }, [doc.path]);
165: 
166:   const meta = useMemo(() => {
167:     if (!result) return null;
168:     const date = frontmatterDate(result.frontmatter?.["date"]) ?? mtime;
169:     const parts = [
170:       ...(date ? [formatDate(date)] : []),
171:       `${result.words.toLocaleString("en-US")} words`,
172:       `${readingMinutes(result.words)} min`,
173:     ];
174:     return parts.join(" � ");
175:   }, [result, mtime]);
176: 
177:   /* ------------------------------------------------------------ the dom */
178: 
179:   // The rendered html is written by hand rather than through
180:   // `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`: React re-applies that on every update it sees,
181:   // which would wipe the anchors, highlighting and diagrams added below.
182:   // Effects run in order, so the html is always in before `enhance` runs.
183:   useEffect(() => {
184:     const content = body.current;
185:     if (content) content.innerHTML = html;
186:   }, [html]);
187: 
188:   useEffect(() => {
189:     const content = body.current;
190:     if (!content || !scopeReady || html === "") return;
191:     const stop = enhance(content, { dir, theme });
192:     trackHeading();
193:     return stop;
194:   }, [html, dir, theme, scopeReady, trackHeading]);
195: 
196:   const scrollToId = useCallback(
197:     (id: string) => {
198:       const frame = scroller.current;
199:       const content = body.current;
200:       if (!frame || !content || id === "") return false;
201:       const target = content.querySelector(`[id="${CSS.escape(id)}"]`);
202:       if (!(target instanceof HTMLElement)) return false;
203:       frame.scrollTop += target.getBoundingClientRect().top - frame.getBoundingClientRect().top - 8;
204:       trackHeading();
205:       return true;
206:     },
207:     [trackHeading],
208:   );
209: 
210:   // Restore the offset for this document, or jump to the heading a link asked
211:   // for before the file was open.
212:   useEffect(() => {
213:     const frame = scroller.current;
214:     if (!frame) return;
215:     if (pendingAnchor?.id === docId) {
216:       const hash = pendingAnchor.hash;
217:       pendingAnchor = null;
218:       if (scrollToId(hash)) return;
219:     }
220:     const kept = scrollTops.get(docId);
221:     if (kept !== undefined) {
222:       frame.scrollTop = kept;
223:       return;
224:     }
225:     // First time this session: pick up where the last one left off (8).
226:     const heading = doc.path ? readingPosition(doc.path) : null;
227:     if (!heading || !scrollToId(heading)) frame.scrollTop = 0;
228:   }, [docId, doc.path, html, scrollToId]);
229: 
230:   useEffect(() => {
231:     const onGoto = (event: Event) => {
232:       const id = (event as CustomEvent<string>).detail;
233:       if (typeof id === "string") scrollToId(id);
234:     };
235:     const onRerender = () => setNonce((value) => value + 1);
236:     window.addEventListener(GOTO_HEADING, onGoto);
237:     window.addEventListener(RERENDER, onRerender);
238:     return () => {
239:       window.removeEventListener(GOTO_HEADING, onGoto);
240:       window.removeEventListener(RERENDER, onRerender);
241:     };
242:   }, [scrollToId]);
243: 
244:   // Leaving read for edit lands on the heading that was at the top.
245:   useEffect(() => {
246:     return () => {
247:       const state = useStore.getState();
248:       const still = state.docs.find((item) => item.id === docId);
249:       if (state.activeId === docId && still?.mode === "edit") {
250:         emitGotoLine(topLine.current);
251:       }
252:     };
253:   }, [docId]);
254: 
255:   // The chords live in the command registry; read only answers the events.
256:   useEffect(() => {
257:     const onFind = () => {
258:       setFindOpen(true);
259:       setFindFocus((value) => value + 1);
260:     };
261:     // `Ctrl+H` in read means edit, which is where replacing happens (5.1).
262:     const onReplace = () => useStore.getState().setMode("edit");
263:     window.addEventListener(FIND, onFind);
264:     window.addEventListener(REPLACE, onReplace);
265:     return () => {
266:       window.removeEventListener(FIND, onFind);
267:       window.removeEventListener(REPLACE, onReplace);
268:     };
269:   }, []);
270: 
271:   /* -------------------------------------------------------------- links */
272: 
273:   const targetOf = useCallback(
274:     (link: HTMLAnchorElement): LinkTarget => {
275:       const wiki = link.dataset["wiki"];
276:       if (wiki !== undefined) {
277:         return resolveWikiLink(wiki, link.dataset["wikiHash"] ?? null, dir);
278:       }
279:       return resolveHref(link.getAttribute("href") ?? "", dir);
280:     },
281:     [dir],
282:   );
283: 
284:   const follow = useCallback(
285:     (link: HTMLAnchorElement, background: boolean) => {
286:       if (link.classList.contains("is-missing")) return;
287:       const target = targetOf(link);
288:       if (target.kind === "external") {
289:         void openExternal(target.url);
290:       } else if (target.kind === "anchor") {
291:         scrollToId(target.id);
292:       } else if (target.kind === "file") {
293:         const path = target.path;
294:         const hash = target.hash;
295:         void (background ? openPathsInBackground([path]) : openPaths([path])).then((opened) => {
296:           if (!opened || background) return;
297:           const id = useStore.getState().activeId;
298:           if (hash && id) pendingAnchor = { id, hash };
299:         });
300:       }
301:     },
302:     [targetOf, scrollToId],
303:   );
304: 
305:   const onClick = (event: MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
306:     const node = event.target as HTMLElement;
307: 
308:     const placeholder = node.closest<HTMLElement>(".img-holder");
309:     if (placeholder) {
310:       event.preventDefault();
311:       void revealImage(placeholder);
312:       return;
313:     }
314: 
315:     const anchor = node.closest<HTMLElement>(".h-anchor");
316:     if (anchor) {
317:       event.preventDefault();
318:       void copy(`#${anchor.dataset["slug"] ?? ""}`);
319:       useStore.getState().setMessage(`copied #${anchor.dataset["slug"] ?? ""}`);
320:       return;
321:     }
322: 
323:     const copyButton = node.closest<HTMLElement>(".code-copy");
324:     if (copyButton) {
325:       event.preventDefault();
326:       const code = copyButton.closest(".codeblock")?.querySelector("code");
327:       void copy(code?.textContent ?? "");
328:       useStore.getState().setMessage("copied");
329:       return;
330:     }
331: 
332:     const link = node.closest("a");
333:     if (link instanceof HTMLAnchorElement) {
334:       event.preventDefault();
335:       follow(link, event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey);
336:       return;
337:     }
338: 
339:     const image = node.closest("img");
340:     if (image instanceof HTMLImageElement && image.dataset["path"]) {
341:       event.preventDefault();
342:       void openInViewer(image.dataset["path"]);
343:     }
344:   };
345: 
346:   const onHover = (event: MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
347:     const link = (event.target as HTMLElement).closest("a");
348:     if (!(link instanceof HTMLAnchorElement)) return;
349:     const target = targetOf(link);
350:     const label =
351:       target.kind === "external"
352:         ? target.url
353:         : target.kind === "anchor"
354:           ? `#${target.id}`
355:           : target.kind === "file"
356:             ? shortPath(target.path, libraryPath ?? dir) +
357:               (link.classList.contains("is-missing") ? " � not found" : "")
358:             : "";
359:     if (label) useStore.getState().setMessage(label);
360:   };
361: 
362:   /* ------------------------------------------------------------- render */
363: 
364:   return (
365:     <>
366:       {findOpen && (
367:         <FindBar
368:           container={scroller.current}
369:           revision={nonce + html.length}
370:           focusToken={findFocus}
371:           onClose={() => setFindOpen(false)}
372:         />
373:       )}
374:       <div
375:         className="content read"
376:         ref={scroller}
377:         onScroll={() => {
378:           const frame = scroller.current;
379:           if (frame) scrollTops.set(docId, frame.scrollTop);
380:           if (ticking.current) return;
381:           ticking.current = true;
382:           requestAnimationFrame(() => {
383:             ticking.current = false;
384:             trackHeading();
385:           });
386:         }}
387:       >
388:         <div className="read-column">
389:           {skip ? (
390:             <div className="read-large">
391:               <p>this file is larger than 2 mb, so it was not rendered.</p>
392:               <button className="link" onClick={() => setForced(true)}>
393:                 render anyway
394:               </button>
395:             </div>
396:           ) : failure ? (
397:             <div className="read-large">
398:               <p>couldn't build this document - {failure}</p>
399:               <button className="link" onClick={() => useStore.getState().setMode("edit")}>
400:                 open the source
401:               </button>
402:             </div>
403:           ) : (
404:             <>
405:               {meta && <div className="read-meta">{meta}</div>}
406:               <div className="read-html" ref={body} onClick={onClick} onMouseOver={onHover} />
407:             </>
408:           )}
409:         </div>
410:       </div>
411:     </>
412:   );
413: }

1: import { emitGotoHeading, emitGotoLine } from "../read/events";
2: import { useActiveHeading } from "../read/outline";

4: 
5: /**
6:  * Headings of the open document, without their `#`, 18px per level
7:  * (spec 4). Read scrolls; edit gets the line through an event, so the two
8:  * views stay unaware of each other.
9:  */
10: export function Outline({ doc }: { doc: Doc | null }) {
11:   const active = useActiveHeading();
12: 
13:   if (!doc || doc.headings.length === 0) {
14:     return <div className="rail-empty">no headings</div>;
15:   }
16: 
17:   return (
18:     <>
19:       {doc.headings.map((heading, index) => (
20:         <button
21:           key={`${heading.id}-${index}`}
22:           className={"outline-item" + (heading.id === active ? " is-active" : "")}
23:           style={{ paddingLeft: `calc(var(--rail-pad-x) + ${(heading.level - 1) * 18}px)` }}
24:           title={heading.text}
25:           onClick={() => {
26:             if (doc.mode === "read") emitGotoHeading(heading.id);
27:             else emitGotoLine(heading.line);
28:           }}
29:         >
30:           {heading.text || "-"}
31:         </button>
32:       ))}
33:     </>
34:   );
35: }

codex

1: // What happens to the rendered HTML once it is in the document: heading
2: // anchors, syntax highlighting, lazy diagrams and images (spec 5.1, 9).
3: //
4: // Every step is idempotent - React's strict mode runs effects twice, and a
5: // second pass must not add a second `#` to every heading.

20: const SAFE_DATA_IMAGE = /^data:image\/(png|jpeg|jpg|gif|webp|avif|bmp|x-icon);/i;
21: 
22: function holder(text: string, action: string, attrs: Record<string, string>): HTMLElement {
23:   const node = document.createElement("span");
24:   node.className = "img-holder";
25:   node.dataset["action"] = action;
26:   for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(attrs)) node.setAttribute(key, value);
27:   node.textContent = text;
28:   return node;
29: }
30: 
31: function hostOf(url: string): string {
32:   try {
33:     return new URL(url).host;
34:   } catch {
35:     return "external";
36:   }
37: }
38: 
39: /** Absolute path of a local image reference, or null when unresolvable. */
40: export function imagePath(raw: string, dir: string | null): string | null {
41:   const path = decode(raw);
42:   if (isAbsolutePath(path)) return path.replace(/\\/g, "/");
43:   if (!dir) return null;
44:   return joinPath(dir, path);
45: }

47: function anchors(root: HTMLElement): void {
48:   for (const heading of root.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>("h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6")) {
49:     if (!heading.id || heading.querySelector(".h-anchor")) continue;
50:     const link = document.createElement("button");
51:     link.type = "button";
52:     link.className = "h-anchor";
53:     link.dataset["slug"] = heading.id;
54:     link.textContent = "#";
55:     heading.prepend(link);
56:   }
57: }
58: 
59: function images(root: HTMLElement, dir: string | null): void {
60:   for (const img of root.querySelectorAll<HTMLImageElement>("img[data-src]")) {
61:     const raw = img.getAttribute("data-src") ?? "";
62:     img.removeAttribute("data-src");
63: 
64:     // Nothing is fetched before the reader asks for it (spec 1.3, rule 2).
65:     if (/^https?:/i.test(raw)) {
66:       img.hidden = true;
67:       img.before(holder(`${hostOf(raw)} � load`, "load", { "data-url": raw }));
68:       continue;
69:     }
70: 
71:     if (/^data:/i.test(raw)) {
72:       if (SAFE_DATA_IMAGE.test(raw)) img.src = raw;
73:       else img.replaceWith(holder("inline image blocked", "none", {}));
74:       continue;
75:     }
76: 
77:     const path = imagePath(raw, dir);
78:     if (!path) {
79:       img.replaceWith(holder("no folder for this image", "none", {}));
80:       continue;
81:     }
82: 
83:     img.dataset["path"] = path;
84: 
85:     if (!isAllowedPath(path)) {
86:       img.hidden = true;
87:       img.before(holder("outside folder � allow", "allow", { "data-dir": folderOf(path) }));
88:       continue;
89:     }
90: 
91:     img.addEventListener(
92:       "error",
93:       () => {
94:         if (img.dataset["failed"]) return;
95:         img.dataset["failed"] = "1";
96:         img.hidden = true;
97:         img.before(holder("couldn't load this image", "none", {}));
98:       },
99:       { once: true },
100:     );
101:     img.src = assetUrl(path);
102:   }
103: }
104: 
105: /** Turns a placeholder back into a picture. Called from the click handler. */
106: export async function revealImage(node: HTMLElement): Promise<void> {
107:   const action = node.dataset["action"];
108:   const img = node.nextElementSibling;
109:   if (!(img instanceof HTMLImageElement)) return;
110: 
111:   if (action === "load") {
112:     img.src = node.getAttribute("data-url") ?? "";
113:   } else if (action === "allow") {
114:     const path = img.dataset["path"];
115:     if (!path) return;
116:     if (!(await allowAssetDir(node.getAttribute("data-dir")))) return;
117:     img.src = assetUrl(path);
118:   } else {
119:     return;
120:   }
121:   img.hidden = false;
122:   node.remove();
123: }
124: 
125: function code(root: HTMLElement): void {
126:   for (const block of root.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>("code[data-lang]")) {
127:     const lang = block.dataset["lang"] ?? "";
128:     if (lang === "" || lang === "mermaid" || block.dataset["hl"]) continue;
129:     const source = block.textContent ?? "";
130:     // The mark goes on only once the markup is in, so a pass that is torn
131:     // down mid-flight (a theme switch) does not leave the block plain.
132:     void highlight(source, lang).then((markup) => {
133:       if (!markup || !block.isConnected || block.dataset["hl"]) return;
134:       block.dataset["hl"] = "1";
135:       block.innerHTML = markup;
136:       block.classList.add("shiki-code");
137:     });
138:   }
139: }
140: 
141: function diagrams(root: HTMLElement, theme: "light" | "dark"): IntersectionObserver | null {
142:   // The mark carries the theme, so a light/dark switch redraws the diagram.
143:   const blocks = [...root.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(".mermaid-block")].filter(
144:     (block) => block.dataset["drawn"] !== theme,
145:   );
146:   if (blocks.length === 0) return null;
147: 
148:   const draw = (block: HTMLElement) => {
149:     if (block.dataset["drawn"] === theme) return;
150:     block.dataset["drawn"] = theme;
151:     void renderDiagram(block, theme);
152:   };
153: 
154:   const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
155:     (entries) => {
156:       for (const entry of entries) {
157:         if (!entry.isIntersecting) continue;
158:         observer.unobserve(entry.target);
159:         draw(entry.target as HTMLElement);
160:       }
161:     },
162:     { rootMargin: "200px" },
163:   );
164: 
165:   for (const block of blocks) {
166:     // A window that is hidden or throttled never gets the observer's first
167:     // record, so whatever is already on screen is drawn right away.
168:     const box = block.getBoundingClientRect();
169:     if (box.top < window.innerHeight + 200 && box.bottom > -200) draw(block);
170:     else observer.observe(block);
171:   }
172:   return observer;
173: }
174: 
175: /**
176:  * A wikilink to a file that is not there is muted and dead (spec 5.1). The
177:  * check is lazy and one file at a time - a document has a handful of them.
178:  */
179: async function wikilinks(
180:   root: HTMLElement,
181:   dir: string | null,
182:   alive: () => boolean,
183: ): Promise<void> {
184:   for (const link of root.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>("a.wikilink")) {
185:     if (link.dataset["checked"]) continue;
186:     link.dataset["checked"] = "1";
187:     const target = resolveWikiLink(link.dataset["wiki"] ?? "", null, dir);
188:     if (target.kind !== "file") {
189:       link.classList.add("is-missing");
190:       continue;
191:     }
192:     link.dataset["path"] = target.path;
193:     if (!inTauri) continue;
194:     // A path we cannot even ask about stays clickable; opening it will say why.
195:     const there = await exists(target.path).catch(() => true);
196:     if (!alive()) return;
197:     if (!there) link.classList.add("is-missing");
198:   }
199: }
200: 
201: /** Runs the whole pass; the returned function stops anything still pending. */
202: export function enhance(root: HTMLElement, options: EnhanceOptions): () => void {
203:   let live = true;
204:   anchors(root);
205:   images(root, options.dir);
206:   code(root);
207:   void wikilinks(root, options.dir, () => live);
208:   const observer = diagrams(root, options.theme);
209: 
210:   return () => {
211:     live = false;
212:     observer?.disconnect();
213:   };
214: }
FILE src/read/wikilink.ts
1: // Wikilinks: [[file]], [[file|alias]], [[file#Heading]] (spec 11).
2: //
3: // A micromark construct, not a regex over the text: `[[x]]` inside a code
4: // span or a fenced block must stay literal, and the construct has to be tried
5: // before micromark's own link-label start on `[`. Extensions merged without
6: // `add: "after"` land in front of the existing ones, which is what we want.
7: 
8: import { markdownLineEnding, markdownSpace } from "micromark-util-character";
9: import { codes } from "micromark-util-symbol";
10: import type {
11:   Code,
12:   Construct,
13:   Effects,
14:   Extension as MicromarkExtension,
15:   State,
16:   TokenizeContext,
17: } from "micromark-util-types";
18: import type {
19:   CompileContext,
20:   Extension as FromMarkdownExtension,
21:   Token,
22: } from "mdast-util-from-markdown";
23: import type { Root } from "mdast";
24: import type { Plugin } from "unified";
25: import type { Data, Node } from "unist";
26: 
27: declare module "micromark-util-types" {
28:   interface TokenTypeMap {
29:     wikiLink: "wikiLink";
30:     wikiLinkMarker: "wikiLinkMarker";
31:     wikiLinkDividerMarker: "wikiLinkDividerMarker";
32:     wikiLinkTarget: "wikiLinkTarget";
33:     wikiLinkHash: "wikiLinkHash";
34:     wikiLinkAlias: "wikiLinkAlias";
35:   }
36: }
37: 
38: export interface WikiLink extends Node {
39:   type: "wikiLink";
40:   /** Everything before `#` or `|`, verbatim. */
41:   target: string;
42:   /** Heading part after `#`, or null. */
43:   hash: string | null;
44:   /** Display text after `|`, or null. */
45:   alias: string | null;
46:   data?: Data;
47: }
48: 
49: function tokenize(this: TokenizeContext, effects: Effects, ok: State, nok: State): State {
50:   // A target of only whitespace (`[[ ]]`) is not a link.
51:   let solid = false;
52: 
53:   return start;
54: 
55:   function start(code: Code): State | undefined {
56:     effects.enter("wikiLink");
57:     effects.enter("wikiLinkMarker");
58:     effects.consume(code);
59:     return open;
60:   }
61: 
62:   function open(code: Code): State | undefined {
63:     if (code !== codes.leftSquareBracket) return nok(code);
64:     effects.consume(code);
65:     effects.exit("wikiLinkMarker");
66:     effects.enter("wikiLinkTarget");
67:     return target;
68:   }
69: 
70:   function target(code: Code): State | undefined {
71:     if (code === codes.eof || markdownLineEnding(code)) return nok(code);
72:     if (code === codes.leftSquareBracket) return nok(code);
73:     if (code === codes.numberSign || code === codes.verticalBar) {
74:       if (!solid) return nok(code);
75:       effects.exit("wikiLinkTarget");
76:       return divider(code);
77:     }
78:     if (code === codes.rightSquareBracket) {
79:       if (!solid) return nok(code);
80:       effects.exit("wikiLinkTarget");
81:       return closeStart(code);
82:     }
83:     if (!markdownSpace(code)) solid = true;
84:     effects.consume(code);
85:     return target;
86:   }
87: 
88:   /** `#` opens the heading part, `|` the alias; both are one-character. */
89:   function divider(code: Code): State | undefined {
90:     const hash = code === codes.numberSign;
91:     effects.enter("wikiLinkDividerMarker");
92:     effects.consume(code);
93:     effects.exit("wikiLinkDividerMarker");
94:     effects.enter(hash ? "wikiLinkHash" : "wikiLinkAlias");
95:     return hash ? inHash : inAlias;
96:   }
97: 
98:   function inHash(code: Code): State | undefined {
99:     if (code === codes.eof || markdownLineEnding(code)) return nok(code);
100:     if (code === codes.leftSquareBracket) return nok(code);
101:     if (code === codes.verticalBar) {
102:       effects.exit("wikiLinkHash");
103:       return divider(code);
104:     }
105:     if (code === codes.rightSquareBracket) {
106:       effects.exit("wikiLinkHash");
107:       return closeStart(code);
108:     }
109:     effects.consume(code);
110:     return inHash;
111:   }
112: 
113:   function inAlias(code: Code): State | undefined {
114:     if (code === codes.eof || markdownLineEnding(code)) return nok(code);
115:     if (code === codes.leftSquareBracket) return nok(code);
116:     if (code === codes.rightSquareBracket) {
117:       effects.exit("wikiLinkAlias");
118:       return closeStart(code);
119:     }
120:     effects.consume(code);
121:     return inAlias;
122:   }
123: 
124:   function closeStart(code: Code): State | undefined {
125:     effects.enter("wikiLinkMarker");
126:     effects.consume(code);
127:     return closeEnd;
128:   }
129: 
130:   function closeEnd(code: Code): State | undefined {
131:     if (code !== codes.rightSquareBracket) return nok(code);
132:     effects.consume(code);
133:     effects.exit("wikiLinkMarker");
134:     effects.exit("wikiLink");
135:     return ok;
136:   }
137: }
138: 
139: const construct: Construct = { name: "wikiLink", tokenize };
140: 
141: export function wikiLinkSyntax(): MicromarkExtension {
142:   return { text: { [codes.leftSquareBracket]: construct } };
143: }
144: 
145: /** What the link shows when it has no alias. */
146: export function displayText(link: Pick<WikiLink, "target" | "hash" | "alias">): string {
147:   if (link.alias) return link.alias;
148:   return link.hash ? `${link.target} > ${link.hash}` : link.target;
149: }
150: 
151: function top(context: CompileContext): WikiLink {
152:   return context.stack[context.stack.length - 1] as unknown as WikiLink;
153: }
154: 
155: export function wikiLinkFromMarkdown(): FromMarkdownExtension {
156:   return {
157:     enter: {
158:       wikiLink(this: CompileContext, token: Token) {
159:         const node: WikiLink = { type: "wikiLink", target: "", hash: null, alias: null };
160:         this.enter(node as never, token);
161:       },
162:     },
163:     exit: {
164:       wikiLinkTarget(this: CompileContext, token: Token) {
165:         top(this).target = this.sliceSerialize(token);
166:       },
167:       wikiLinkHash(this: CompileContext, token: Token) {
168:         top(this).hash = this.sliceSerialize(token);
169:       },
170:       wikiLinkAlias(this: CompileContext, token: Token) {
171:         top(this).alias = this.sliceSerialize(token);
172:       },
173:       wikiLink(this: CompileContext, token: Token) {
174:         const node = top(this);
175:         // The path is resolved in the DOM (a missing file is muted and dead),
176:         // so the target travels as data-* and the element gets no href here.
177:         node.data = {
178:           hName: "a",
179:           hProperties: {
180:             className: ["wikilink"],
181:             dataWiki: node.target,
182:             ...(node.hash ? { dataWikiHash: node.hash } : {}),
183:           },
184:           hChildren: [{ type: "text", value: displayText(node) }],
185:         };
186:         this.exit(token);
187:       },
188:     },
189:   };
190: }

192: /** unified plugin: syntax extension + mdast handlers. */
193: export const remarkWikiLink: Plugin<[], Root> = function () {
194:   const data = this.data();
195:   const micromark = data.micromarkExtensions ?? (data.micromarkExtensions = []);
196:   const fromMarkdown = data.fromMarkdownExtensions ?? (data.fromMarkdownExtensions = []);
197:   micromark.push(wikiLinkSyntax());
198:   fromMarkdown.push(wikiLinkFromMarkdown());
199: };
FILE src/read/callout.ts
1: // Callouts and GitHub alerts, one implementation (spec 11):
2: //
3: //   > [!note]
4: //   > body
5: //
6: //   > [!WARNING] Mind the gap
7: //   > body
8: //
9: // A blockquote whose first line is `[!type]` becomes a callout: uppercase
10: // label, left rule, no icons. Done on mdast rather than in micromark - the
11: // construct is a blockquote with a marker line, and rewriting the tree keeps
12: // nested content (lists, code, other quotes) working for free.
13: 
14: import { visit } from "unist-util-visit";
15: import type { Blockquote, PhrasingContent, Root, RootContent } from "mdast";
16: 
17: const MARKER = /^\[!([A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_-]*)\][+-]?[ \t]*/;
18: 
19: /** Splits a paragraph's children at the first line break. */
20: function splitAtLineEnd(
21:   children: PhrasingContent[],
22: ): [PhrasingContent[], PhrasingContent[]] {
23:   const head: PhrasingContent[] = [];
24: 
25:   for (let i = 0; i < children.length; i += 1) {
26:     const child = children[i];
27:     if (!child) continue;
28:     if (child.type === "break") return [head, children.slice(i + 1)];
29:     if (child.type === "text") {
30:       const at = child.value.indexOf("\n");
31:       if (at >= 0) {
32:         const before = child.value.slice(0, at);
33:         const after = child.value.slice(at + 1);
34:         if (before) head.push({ ...child, value: before });
35:         const tail: PhrasingContent[] = [];
36:         if (after) tail.push({ ...child, value: after });
37:         tail.push(...children.slice(i + 1));
38:         return [head, tail];
39:       }
40:     }
41:     head.push(child);
42:   }
43: 
44:   return [head, []];
45: }
46: 
47: function isBlank(children: PhrasingContent[]): boolean {
48:   return children.every((child) => child.type === "text" && child.value.trim() === "");
49: }
50: 
51: function span(className: string, children: PhrasingContent[]): RootContent {
52:   return {
53:     type: "calloutSpan",
54:     data: { hName: "span", hProperties: { className: [className] } },
55:     children,
56:   } as unknown as RootContent;
57: }
58: 
59: export function remarkCallouts() {
60:   return (tree: Root): undefined => {
61:     visit(tree, "blockquote", (node: Blockquote) => {
62:       const first = node.children[0];
63:       if (!first || first.type !== "paragraph") return;
64:       const lead = first.children[0];
65:       if (!lead || lead.type !== "text") return;
66: 
67:       const match = MARKER.exec(lead.value);
68:       const kind = match?.[1];
69:       if (!match || !kind) return;
70: 
71:       lead.value = lead.value.slice(match[0].length);
72:       const [title, body] = splitAtLineEnd(first.children);
73: 
74:       if (body.length > 0) first.children = body;
75:       else node.children.shift();
76: 
77:       const label: PhrasingContent[] = [{ type: "text", value: kind.toUpperCase() }];
78:       const parts: RootContent[] = [span("callout-type", label)];
79:       if (!isBlank(title)) {
80:         const trimmed = [...title];
81:         const start = trimmed[0];
82:         if (start?.type === "text") trimmed[0] = { ...start, value: start.value.trimStart() };
83:         parts.push(span("callout-heading", trimmed));
84:       }
85: 
86:       node.children.unshift({
87:         type: "calloutTitle",
88:         data: { hName: "div", hProperties: { className: ["callout-title"] } },
89:         children: parts,
90:       } as unknown as Blockquote["children"][number]);
91: 
92:       node.data = {
93:         ...node.data,
94:         hName: "div",
95:         hProperties: { className: ["callout"], dataCallout: kind.toLowerCase() },
96:       };
97:     });
98:     return undefined;
99:   };
100: }
FILE src/read/words.ts
1: // Word count for the status bar and the meta line above H1 (spec 5.1).
2: // Front matter and fenced code are not prose, so they never reach the
3: // segmenter. Everything else - including inline code and link text - counts.
4: 
5: const segmenter =
6:   typeof Intl !== "undefined" && "Segmenter" in Intl
7:     ? new Intl.Segmenter(undefined, { granularity: "word" })
8:     : null;
9: 
10: const FENCE = /^ {0,3}(`{3,}|~{3,})/;
11: 
12: /** Drops YAML front matter and fenced code blocks; keeps the rest verbatim. */
13: export function prose(text: string): string {
14:   const lines = text.replace(/^?/, "").split(/\r?\n/);
15:   let i = 0;
16: 
17:   // Front matter only exists when `---` is the very first line (spec 11).
18:   if (lines[0]?.trim() === "---") {
19:     let end = 1;
20:     while (end < lines.length && !/^(-{3,}|\.{3})\s*$/.test(lines[end] ?? "")) end += 1;
21:     if (end < lines.length) i = end + 1;
22:   }
23: 
24:   const out: string[] = [];
25:   let fence: string | null = null;
26: 
27:   for (; i < lines.length; i += 1) {
28:     const line = lines[i] ?? "";
29:     const match = FENCE.exec(line);
30:     if (fence === null) {
31:       if (match?.[1]) {
32:         fence = match[1];
33:         continue;
34:       }
35:       out.push(line);
36:       continue;
37:     }
38:     // A closing fence is the same character, at least as long, nothing after.
39:     const marker = match?.[1];
40:     if (
41:       marker &&
42:       marker[0] === fence[0] &&
43:       marker.length >= fence.length &&
44:       line.slice(match[0].length).trim() === ""
45:     ) {
46:       fence = null;
47:     }
48:   }
49: 
50:   return out.join("\n");
51: }
52: 
53: /** Word-like segments of the prose part of a markdown document. */
54: export function countWords(text: string): number {
55:   const body = prose(text);
56:   if (!segmenter) return body.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length;
57:   let count = 0;
58:   for (const part of segmenter.segment(body)) if (part.isWordLike) count += 1;
59:   return count;
60: }
61: 
62: /** Reading time in whole minutes, at the 200 wpm of spec 5.1. */
63: export function readingMinutes(words: number): number {
64:   return Math.max(1, Math.round(words / 200));
65: }
FILE src/read/mermaid.ts
1: // Mermaid, imported only when a diagram actually scrolls into view
2: // (spec 5.1). A broken diagram shows its source plus the error line.
3: 
4: let mermaid: Promise<typeof import("mermaid").default> | null = null;
5: let configured: "light" | "dark" | null = null;
6: let seq = 0;
7: 
8: async function load(theme: "light" | "dark") {
9:   mermaid ??= import("mermaid").then((module) => module.default);
10:   const instance = await mermaid;
11:   if (configured !== theme) {
12:     instance.initialize({
13:       startOnLoad: false,
14:       securityLevel: "strict",
15:       theme: theme === "dark" ? "dark" : "neutral",
16:       fontFamily: '"IBM Plex Mono", ui-monospace, Menlo, monospace',
17:       fontSize: 12,
18:     });
19:     configured = theme;
20:   }
21:   return instance;
22: }
23: 
24: function reason(error: unknown): string {
25:   const text = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
26:   return text.split("\n").slice(0, 2).join(" ").trim() || "diagram failed";
27: }
28: 
29: /**
30:  * Replaces the source of `block` with an SVG. On failure the source stays and
31:  * the message is appended, so nothing is silently lost.
32:  */
33: export async function renderDiagram(
34:   block: HTMLElement,
35:   theme: "light" | "dark",
36: ): Promise<void> {
37:   const source = block.querySelector("pre")?.textContent ?? "";
38:   if (source.trim() === "") return;
39: 
40:   try {
41:     const instance = await load(theme);
42:     seq += 1;
43:     const { svg } = await instance.render(`plain-mermaid-${seq}`, source);
44:     const holder = document.createElement("div");
45:     holder.className = "mermaid-svg";
46:     holder.innerHTML = svg;
47:     block.querySelector("pre")?.setAttribute("hidden", "");
48:     block.querySelector(".mermaid-svg")?.remove();
49:     block.append(holder);
50:   } catch (error) {
51:     block.querySelector(".mermaid-error")?.remove();
52:     const note = document.createElement("div");
53:     note.className = "mermaid-error";
54:     note.textContent = reason(error);
55:     block.append(note);
56:   }
57: }
FILE src/editor/headings.ts
1: // Heading slugs for read <-> edit jumps (spec 5.0). Read builds the same
2: // ids with rehype-slug; both run one slugger over the document top to bottom
3: // so duplicate headings get the same `-1` suffix on either side.
4: 
5: import GithubSlugger from "github-slugger";
6: 
7: export interface SourceHeading {
8:   /** 1-based line in the source. */
9:   line: number;
10:   text: string;
11:   id: string;
12: }
13: 
14: const FENCE = /^\s{0,3}(```|~~~)/;
15: const ATX = /^\s{0,3}(#{1,6})\s+(.*?)\s*#*\s*$/;
16: 
17: /** Drops link and image syntax so the slug matches the rendered heading. */
18: function plain(text: string): string {
19:   return text.replace(/!?\[([^\]]*)\]\([^)]*\)/g, "$1").replace(/!?\[\[([^\]|]*)(?:\|[^\]]*)?\]\]/g, "$1");
20: }
21: 
22: export function headings(text: string): SourceHeading[] {
23:   const slugger = new GithubSlugger();
24:   const found: SourceHeading[] = [];
25:   let fenced = false;
26:   const lines = text.split(/\r\n|\r|\n/);
27:   for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i += 1) {
28:     const line = lines[i] ?? "";
29:     if (FENCE.test(line)) {
30:       fenced = !fenced;
31:       continue;
32:     }
33:     if (fenced) continue;
34:     const match = ATX.exec(line);
35:     if (!match) continue;
36:     const title = plain(match[2] ?? "");
37:     found.push({ line: i + 1, text: title, id: slugger.slug(title) });
38:   }
39:   return found;
40: }
41: 
42: /** The heading a given line belongs to, for "same heading" scrolling. */
43: export function headingAt(text: string, line: number): SourceHeading | null {
44:   let current: SourceHeading | null = null;
45:   for (const heading of headings(text)) {
46:     if (heading.line > line) break;
47:     current = heading;
48:   }
49:   return current;
50: }
FILE src/editor/toolbar.ts
1: // The floating format panel (spec 5.2, mockup 1d): a dark 30px strip above
2: // a selection made with the mouse. Keyboard selections do not get it - for
3: // those there are chords. One instance, owned by the view that made it.
4: 
5: import type { Extension } from "@codemirror/state";
6: import { EditorView, ViewPlugin, type ViewUpdate } from "@codemirror/view";
7: import {
8:   insertLink,
9:   toggleBold,
10:   toggleInlineCode,
11:   toggleItalic,
12:   toggleQuote,
13:   toggleStrike,
14: } from "./commands";
15: 
16: /** Mockup 1d: the strip sits 38px above the top of the selection. */
17: const OFFSET = 38;
18: 
19: interface Button {
20:   label: string;
21:   title: string;
22:   className: string;
23:   run: (view: EditorView) => boolean;
24: }
25: 
26: const BUTTONS: Button[] = [
27:   { label: "B", title: "bold", className: "tb-bold", run: toggleBold },
28:   { label: "I", title: "italic", className: "tb-italic", run: toggleItalic },
29:   { label: "S", title: "strikethrough", className: "tb-strike", run: toggleStrike },
30:   { label: "`", title: "code", className: "tb-code", run: toggleInlineCode },
31:   { label: "link", title: "link", className: "", run: insertLink },
32:   { label: "quote", title: "quote", className: "", run: toggleQuote },
33: ];
34: 
35: /** `link` and `quote` sit behind the separator, as in the mockup. */
36: const SEPARATOR_BEFORE = 4;
37: 
38: class FormatToolbar {
39:   private dom: HTMLElement | null = null;
40:   /** Only the newest pending mouseup may place the panel. */
41:   private token = 0;
42:   /** A click on a button ends in a mouseup that must not bring it back. */
43:   private suppress = false;
44: 
45:   constructor(private view: EditorView) {
46:     view.dom.addEventListener("mouseup", this.onMouseUp);
47:     view.scrollDOM.addEventListener("scroll", this.hide);
48:     view.dom.addEventListener("focusout", this.onFocusOut);
49:   }
50: 
51:   update(update: ViewUpdate): void {
52:     // Typing, moving the caret and dragging a selection all take it away;
53:     // the mouseup that ends a drag is what brings it back.
54:     if (update.docChanged || update.selectionSet || update.focusChanged) this.hide();
55:   }
56: 
57:   destroy(): void {
58:     this.view.dom.removeEventListener("mouseup", this.onMouseUp);
59:     this.view.scrollDOM.removeEventListener("scroll", this.hide);
60:     this.view.dom.removeEventListener("focusout", this.onFocusOut);
61:     this.hide();
62:   }
63: 
64:   private onFocusOut = (event: FocusEvent): void => {
65:     // Clicking a button moves the focus inside the panel; that is not a blur.
66:     const next = event.relatedTarget;
67:     if (next instanceof Node && this.dom?.contains(next)) return;
68:     this.hide();
69:   };
70: 
71:   // CodeMirror settles the selection in its own mouseup handler, so the panel
72:   // is placed one task later, when the selection is the final one.
73:   private onMouseUp = (): void => {
74:     if (this.suppress) {
75:       this.suppress = false;
76:       return;
77:     }
78:     const token = ++this.token;
79:     window.setTimeout(() => {
80:       if (token === this.token) this.show();
81:     }, 0);
82:   };
83: 
84:   private hide = (): void => {
85:     this.token += 1;
86:     this.dom?.remove();
87:     this.dom = null;
88:   };
89: 
90:   private build(): HTMLElement {
91:     const dom = document.createElement("div");
92:     dom.className = "format-toolbar";
93:     BUTTONS.forEach((button, index) => {
94:       if (index === SEPARATOR_BEFORE) {
95:         const separator = document.createElement("span");
96:         separator.className = "tb-sep";
97:         dom.append(separator);
98:       }
99:       const element = document.createElement("button");
100:       if (button.className) element.className = button.className;
101:       element.title = button.title;
102:       element.textContent = button.label;
103:       // mousedown, not click: the default would take the selection away
104:       // before the command has anything to work on.
105:       element.addEventListener("mousedown", (event) => {
106:         event.preventDefault();
107:         this.suppress = true;
108:         this.hide();
109:         button.run(this.view);
110:         this.view.focus();
111:       });
112:       dom.append(element);
113:     });
114:     return dom;
115:   }
116: 
117:   private show(): void {
118:     const { main } = this.view.state.selection;
119:     if (main.empty || !this.view.hasFocus) return;
120: 
121:     const coords = this.view.coordsAtPos(main.from);
122:     if (!coords) return;
123: 
124:     const dom = this.dom ?? this.build();
125:     if (!dom.isConnected) this.view.dom.append(dom);
126:     this.dom = dom;
127: 
128:     // coordsAtPos is in client space; the panel is absolute inside .cm-editor.
129:     const host = this.view.dom.getBoundingClientRect();
130:     const left = Math.min(
131:       Math.max(0, coords.left - host.left),
132:       Math.max(0, host.width - dom.offsetWidth),
133:     );
134:     dom.style.left = `${Math.round(left)}px`;
135:     dom.style.top = `${Math.round(coords.top - host.top - OFFSET)}px`;
136:   }
137: }
138: 
139: export const formatToolbar: Extension = ViewPlugin.fromClass(FormatToolbar);

# ТЗ · Plain — Markdown reader/editor для Windows, личная редакция

| Поле | Значение |

История: v1.2 полная (`archive/`), v2.0 компактная; разбор сокращений — `review/overengineering-pass.md`, отзывы Codex — `review/codex-review-1…4*.md`. Осознанно оставлено вопреки последнему отзыву: grep-поиск по папке в v0.1 (это причина иметь папку вообще) и запоминание позиций чтения (просто JSON) |

| Оценка объёма v0.1 | 12–18 недель по ~10 часов в неделю (оценка Codex после сокращений; с освоением Rust/Tauri — +25%) |
