src/app/watcher.ts
1: // External changes (spec §8). Rust reports raw paths; only this side knows
2: // the hash a buffer came from, so the whole decision lives here.
3: 
4: import { listen } from "@tauri-apps/api/event";
5: import { isDirty } from "../editor/buffers";
6: import { inTauri } from "./env";
7: import { hashFile, watchPaths } from "./fs";
8: import { pathKey } from "./paths";
9: import { reloadFromDisk, showConflict } from "./save";
10: import { useStore } from "./store";
11: 
12: /** Wave 5 hangs the file tree off this. */
13: export const TREE_CHANGED = "plain:tree-changed";
14: 
15: const checking = new Set<string>();
16: 
17: /**
18:  * Same hash as the last write of ours -> our own change, ignore it.
19:  * Different and the buffer is clean -> reload quietly. Different with edits
20:  * -> the conflict banner, which is the only thing that may lose work.
21:  */
22: export async function checkDoc(id: string): Promise<void> {
23:   if (checking.has(id)) return;
24:   checking.add(id);
25:   try {
26:     const store = useStore.getState();
27:     const doc = store.docs.find((d) => d.id === id);
28:     if (!doc?.path) return;
29: 
30:     const hash = await hashFile(doc.path);
31:     if (hash === null) {
32:       if (!doc.deleted) store.updateDoc(id, { deleted: true });
33:       return;
34:     }
35:     if (doc.deleted) store.updateDoc(id, { deleted: false });
36:     if (hash === doc.baseHash) return;
37:     if (isDirty(id, doc.text)) {
38:       showConflict(id);
39:       return;
40:     }
41:     await reloadFromDisk(id);
42:   } finally {
43:     checking.delete(id);
44:   }
45: }
46: 
47: async function onChange(paths: string[]): Promise<void> {
48:   let tree = false;
49:   for (const path of paths) {
50:     const key = pathKey(path);
51:     const doc = useStore.getState().docs.find((d) => d.path && pathKey(d.path) === key);
52:     if (doc) await checkDoc(doc.id);
53:     else tree = true;
54:   }
55:   if (tree) window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(TREE_CHANGED));
56: }
57: 
58: /** One watcher; changing the library or opening a file restarts it. */
59: let armed = "";
60: 
61: function arm(): void {
62:   const state = useStore.getState();
63:   const files = state.docs.map((d) => d.path).filter((p): p is string => p !== null);
64:   const key = `${state.libraryPath ?? ""}|${files.join("|")}`;
65:   if (key === armed) return;
66:   armed = key;
67:   void watchPaths(state.libraryPath, files).catch(() => undefined);
68: }
69: 
70: export function installWatcher(): () => void {
71:   if (!inTauri) return () => undefined;
72: 
73:   const unsubscribe = useStore.subscribe((state, previous) => {
74:     if (state.docs !== previous.docs || state.libraryPath !== previous.libraryPath) arm();
75:   });
76:   arm();
77: 
78:   const unlisten = listen<{ paths: string[] }>("fs-change", (event) => {
79:     void onChange(event.payload.paths ?? []);
80:   });
81: 
82:   // A file changed while Plain was in the background often produces no event
83:   // at all on network folders, so coming back also checks (spec §8).
84:   const onFocus = () => {
85:     const { activeId } = useStore.getState();
86:     if (activeId) void checkDoc(activeId);
87:   };
88:   window.addEventListener("focus", onFocus);
89: 
90:   return () => {
91:     unsubscribe();
92:     void unlisten.then((off) => off());
93:     window.removeEventListener("focus", onFocus);
94:     // A remount (React strict mode, a hot reload) has to arm it again.
95:     armed = "";
96:   };
97: }
98: 

src/app/save.ts
1: // Ctrl+S and everything around it (spec §8). The rules, in order:
2: // a clean document is never written; the snapshot that goes to disk is taken
3: // before the write and compared to the buffer after it; the base hash always
4: // follows a successful write; nothing the user did not touch changes.
5: 
6: import { save as saveDialog } from "@tauri-apps/plugin-dialog";
7: import { flushActiveEditor, isDirty, markSaved, renameBuffer, replaceText } from "../editor";
8: import { dropBuffer } from "../editor/buffers";
9: import { dropDraft, writeDraft } from "./drafts";
10: import { inTauri } from "./env";
11: import { encodingLabel, isLegacy, normalizeEol, serialize } from "./eol";
12: import { docFields, fsError, readFile, writeFileAtomic } from "./fs";
13: import { dirname, pathKey } from "./paths";
14: import { activeDoc, useStore, type Doc } from "./store";
15: 
16: /* -------------------------------------------------------------- plumbing */
17: 
18: /**
19:  * Writes of one document never overlap: a second Ctrl+S waits for the first
20:  * (spec §8). Rust serializes writes too; this keeps the store consistent.
21:  */
22: const queues = new Map<string, Promise<unknown>>();
23: 
24: function queued<T>(id: string, run: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
25:   const previous = queues.get(id) ?? Promise.resolve();
26:   const next = previous.then(run, run);
27:   queues.set(id, next);
28:   void next.catch(() => undefined).then(() => {
29:     if (queues.get(id) === next) queues.delete(id);
30:   });
31:   return next;
32: }
33: 
34: function doc(id: string): Doc | undefined {
35:   return useStore.getState().docs.find((d) => d.id === id);
36: }
37: 
38: /* ------------------------------------------------------------- the write */
39: 
40: /**
41:  * The base hash always follows a successful write, or the next save would
42:  * conflict with our own file (spec §8). Then the buffer is compared to the
43:  * snapshot: unchanged means saved and the draft can go, changed means the
44:  * document is still unsaved and the draft stays with the new base hash.
45:  */
46: function settle(id: string, snapshot: string, hash: string): void {
47:   const store = useStore.getState();
48:   markSaved(id, snapshot);
49:   // Anything typed while the write was in flight counts against the snapshot,
50:   // so the editor is read now rather than on its next idle callback.
51:   flushActiveEditor();
52:   const current = doc(id);
53:   if (!current) return;
54:   const dirty = normalizeEol(current.text) !== snapshot;
55:   store.updateDoc(id, { savedText: snapshot, baseHash: hash, dirty, deleted: false });
56:   store.dismissBanner("conflict");
57:   store.dismissBanner("save-failed");
58:   if (dirty) void writeDraft(id);
59:   else void dropDraft(current);
60: }
61: 
62: interface Target {
63:   path: string;
64:   baseHash: string | null;
65:   allowMissing?: boolean;
66: }
67: 
68: async function write(id: string, target: Target): Promise<boolean> {
69:   const current = doc(id);
70:   if (!current) return false;
71:   const snapshot = normalizeEol(current.text);
72: 
73:   try {
74:     const hash = await writeFileAtomic({
75:       path: target.path,
76:       text: serialize(snapshot, current.eol),
77:       encoding: current.encoding,
78:       bom: current.bom,
79:       eol: current.eol,
80:       baseHash: target.baseHash,
81:       allowMissing: target.allowMissing ?? false,
82:     });
83:     settle(id, snapshot, hash);
84:     // One of the two allowed transformations, and it says so (spec §1.3).
85:     if (current.mixedEol) {
86:       useStore.getState().updateDoc(id, { mixedEol: false });
87:       useStore.getState().setMessage(`line endings normalized to ${current.eol}`);
88:     }
89:     return true;
90:   } catch (error) {
91:     const failure = fsError(error);
92:     if (failure.kind === "conflict") {
93:       showConflict(id);
94:       return false;
95:     }
96:     // Deleted from under us, and the buffer holds edits: put the file back.
97:     if (failure.kind === "missing") {
98:       const back = await write(id, { ...target, allowMissing: true });
99:       if (back) useStore.getState().setNote("recreated");
100:       return back;
101:     }
102:     useStore.getState().showBanner({
103:       id: "save-failed",
104:       text: `couldn't save — ${failure.message}`,
105:       actions: [
106:         { label: "retry", run: () => void save(id) },
107:         { label: "save as…", run: () => void saveAs(id) },
108:       ],
109:     });
110:     return false;
111:   }
112: }
113: 
114: /* ------------------------------------------------------------- commands */
115: 
116: export function saveActive(): Promise<boolean> {
117:   flushActiveEditor();
118:   const current = activeDoc(useStore.getState());
119:   return current ? save(current.id) : Promise.resolve(true);
120: }

src/app/commands.ts
25: 
26:   for (const path of paths) {
27:     // Already open under another spelling of the same path: just show it,
28:     // and do not read the file a second time.
29:     const existing = useStore.getState().docs.find((d) => d.id === pathKey(path));
30:     if (existing) {
31:       activate(existing.id);
32:       opened = true;
33:       continue;
34:     }
35:     try {
36:       openDoc(makeDoc({ id: pathKey(path), path, ...docFields(await readFile(path)) }));
37:       opened = true;
38:     } catch (error) {
39:       showBanner({
40:         id: "open-failed",
41:         text: `couldn't open ${basename(path)} — ${fsError(error).message}`,
42:         actions: [{ label: "dismiss", run: () => useStore.getState().dismissBanner("open-failed") }],
43:       });
44:     }
45:   }
46:   return opened;
47: }
48: 
49: /**
50:  * `Ctrl+N`. With a library open the tree grows a row with an inline name
51:  * field in the root folder; without one it is a nameless buffer whose
52:  * `Ctrl+S` is Save As (spec §6).
53:  */
54: let untitled = 0;
55: 
56: export function newDoc(): void {
57:   const store = useStore.getState();
58:   if (store.libraryPath) {
59:     store.setRailCollapsed(false);
60:     store.setRailView("files");
61:     store.setTreeDraft({ parent: store.libraryPath, kind: "file" });
62:     return;
63:   }
64:   untitled += 1;
65:   store.openDoc(
66:     makeDoc({
67:       id: `untitled-${untitled}`,
68:       path: null,
69:       text: "",
70:       mode: "edit",
71:       eol: store.settings.files.newFileEol,
72:     }),
73:   );
74: }
75: 
76: /** `F5`: build the document again, and re-read it when it is clean. */
77: export function refresh(): void {
78:   const doc = activeDoc(useStore.getState());
79:   if (!doc?.path) {
80:     emitRerender();
81:     return;
82:   }
83:   if (isDirty(doc.id, doc.text)) {
84:     useStore.getState().setMessage("unsaved changes — save or reload from banner");
85:     emitRerender();
86:     return;
87:   }
88:   void reloadFromDisk(doc.id, "").then(emitRerender);
89: }
90: 
91: /**
92:  * Takes whatever Rust parked for us — launch arguments, or the argv of a
93:  * second launch. Opening a file this way collapses the rail (spec §6).
94:  */
95: export async function pendingPaths(): Promise<string[]> {
96:   return inTauri ? invoke<string[]>("take_pending_paths") : [];
97: }
98: 
99: /** Folder arguments, parked separately: a folder is a library (spec §6). */
100: export async function pendingFolders(): Promise<string[]> {
101:   return inTauri ? invoke<string[]>("take_pending_folders") : [];
102: }
103: 
104: export async function drainPendingPaths(): Promise<void> {
105:   const folders = await pendingFolders();
106:   const first = folders[0];
107:   if (first) await openLibraryPath(first);
108:   const paths = await pendingPaths();
109:   if (paths.length === 0) return;
110:   // A file on its own arrives with the rail out of the way; a file that came
111:   // with its folder does not, because the folder is the point.
112:   if ((await openPaths(paths)) && !first) useStore.getState().setRailCollapsed(true);
113: }
114:

src/ui/FolderSearch.tsx
1: // Search inside the library (spec §7): a screen in the style of the library
2: // instead of the document. One Rust call per question, results grouped by
3: // file, a click lands on the line in edit.
4: 
5: import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
6: import { openInEdit } from "../app/commands";
7: import { basename } from "../app/paths";
8: import { emitGotoLine } from "../read/events";
9: import { useStore } from "../app/store";
10: import { runner, type LineMatch, type SearchAnswer } from "../library/search";
11: import "./library.css";
12: 
13: /** `notes/ideas.md` -> `notes`; a file in the root has nothing to add. */
14: function folderOf(rel: string): string {
15:   const at = rel.lastIndexOf("/");
16:   return at < 0 ? "" : rel.slice(0, at);
17: }
18: 
19: /** The matched parts of a context line, painted with the accent. */
20: function Line({ match }: { match: LineMatch }) {
21:   const parts: React.ReactNode[] = [];
22:   let at = 0;
23:   match.ranges.forEach(([from, to], index) => {
24:     if (from > at) parts.push(match.text.slice(at, from));
25:     parts.push(<mark key={index}>{match.text.slice(from, to)}</mark>);
26:     at = to;
27:   });
28:   if (at < match.text.length) parts.push(match.text.slice(at));
29:   return <span className="folder-search-text">{parts}</span>;
30: }
31: 
32: export function FolderSearch() {
33:   const libraryPath = useStore((s) => s.libraryPath);
34:   const extensions = useStore((s) => s.settings.library.extensions);
35: 
36:   const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
37:   const [caseSensitive, setCaseSensitive] = useState(false);
38:   const [regex, setRegex] = useState(false);
39:   const [answer, setAnswer] = useState<SearchAnswer | null>(null);
40:   const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
41:   const input = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
42: 
43:   const ask = useMemo(
44:     () =>
45:       runner(
46:         (result) => {
47:           setError(null);
48:           setAnswer(result);
49:         },
50:         (message) => {
51:           setError(message);
52:           setAnswer(null);
53:         },
54:       ),
55:     [],
56:   );
57: 
58:   useEffect(() => () => ask.stop(), [ask]);
59:   useEffect(() => input.current?.focus(), []);
60: 
61:   // `Esc` goes back to the document from anywhere on the screen (spec §7).
62:   useEffect(() => {
63:     const onKey = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
64:       if (event.key !== "Escape") return;
65:       event.preventDefault();
66:       useStore.getState().setFolderSearch(false);
67:     };
68:     window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
69:     return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
70:   }, []);
71: 
72:   useEffect(() => {
73:     if (!libraryPath) return;
74:     ask.run({ root: libraryPath, query, caseSensitive, regex, extensions });
75:   }, [ask, libraryPath, query, caseSensitive, regex, extensions]);
76: 
77:   const close = () => useStore.getState().setFolderSearch(false);
78: 
79:   const lines = answer?.files.reduce((total, file) => total + file.matches.length, 0) ?? 0;

src/app/keys.ts
1: // One keyboard layer for the whole app. There are no chords here: every
2: // binding comes from the command registry (spec §12), so a shortcut and the
3: // menu entry that shows it can never drift apart. Tree keys (`F2`, `Delete`,
4: // the arrows) are local to the tree and live in the rail.
5: 
6: import { tinykeys } from "tinykeys";
7: import { chordToPattern, matchesChord } from "./chords";
8: import { commands } from "./registry";
9: 
10: /**
11:  * WebView2 keeps its own accelerators (spec §12 leaves them on for zoom), but
12:  * these must not reach it — they are ours, or they would reload or print.
13:  */
14: const SWALLOW = [
15:   "Ctrl+F",
16:   "Ctrl+P",
17:   "Ctrl+G",
18:   "F5",
19:   "Ctrl+S",
20:   "Ctrl+Shift+S",
21:   "Ctrl+N",
22:   "Ctrl+O",
23:   "Ctrl+H",
24:   "Ctrl+K",
25: ];
26: 
27: /** chord -> action, straight out of the registry. */
28: export function bindings(): Record<string, () => void> {
29:   const map: Record<string, () => void> = {};
30:   for (const command of commands) {
31:     if (!command.chord) continue;
32:     map[command.chord] = () => void command.run();
33:   }
34:   return map;
35: }
36: 
37: export function installKeys(): () => void {
38:   const swallow = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
39:     if (SWALLOW.some((chord) => matchesChord(chord, event))) event.preventDefault();
40:   };
41:   window.addEventListener("keydown", swallow, { capture: true });
42: 
43:   // WebView2 would otherwise show its own context menu (spec §12). Bubble
44:   // phase, so an element that has its own menu can stop this first.
45:   const noMenu = (event: MouseEvent) => event.preventDefault();
46:   window.addEventListener("contextmenu", noMenu);
47: 
48:   const map: Record<string, (event: KeyboardEvent) => void> = {};
49:   for (const [chord, run] of Object.entries(bindings())) {
50:     map[chordToPattern(chord)] = (event) => {
51:       event.preventDefault();
52:       run();
53:     };
54:   }
55: 
56:   const off = tinykeys(window, map, {
57:     // Shortcuts must keep working while the editor (contenteditable) has
58:     // focus, so only auto-repeat and IME composition are skipped.
59:     ignore: (event) => event.repeat || event.isComposing,
60:   });
61: 
62:   return () => {
63:     window.removeEventListener("keydown", swallow, { capture: true });
64:     window.removeEventListener("contextmenu", noMenu);
65:     off();
66:   };
67: }

src/editor/commands.test.ts
1: import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
2: import { EditorSelection, EditorState, type StateCommand } from "@codemirror/state";
3: import { indentUnit } from "@codemirror/language";
4: import { indentLess, indentMore } from "@codemirror/commands";
5: import {
6:   clearHeading,
7:   cycleList,
8:   detectIndent,
9:   insertLink,
10:   listKind,
11:   toggleBold,
12:   toggleCheckbox,
13:   toggleHeading,
14:   toggleItalic,
15:   toggleQuote,
16:   toggleStrike,
17: } from "./commands";
18: 
19: /** Runs a command over a document; `|` marks the caret, `«»` the selection. */
20: function run(command: StateCommand, input: string, unit = "  "): string {
21:   const { doc, selection } = parse(input);
22:   const state = EditorState.create({ doc, selection, extensions: indentUnit.of(unit) });
23:   let next = state;
24:   const ran = command({ state, dispatch: (tr) => (next = tr.state) });
25:   expect(ran).toBe(true);
26:   return print(next);
27: }
28: 
29: function parse(input: string): { doc: string; selection: EditorSelection } {
30:   const start = input.indexOf("«");
31:   if (start >= 0) {
32:     const end = input.indexOf("»") - 1;
33:     return {
34:       doc: input.replace("«", "").replace("»", ""),
35:       selection: EditorSelection.single(start, end),
36:     };
37:   }
38:   const caret = input.indexOf("|");
39:   return {
40:     doc: input.replace("|", ""),
41:     selection: EditorSelection.single(caret < 0 ? 0 : caret),
42:   };
43: }
44: 
45: function print(state: EditorState): string {
46:   const { from, to } = state.selection.main;
47:   const text = state.doc.toString();
48:   return from === to
49:     ? text.slice(0, from) + "|" + text.slice(from)
50:     : text.slice(0, from) + "«" + text.slice(from, to) + "»" + text.slice(to);
51: }
52: 
53: describe("inline marks", () => {
54:   it("wraps the selection and puts the marks back off", () => {
55:     expect(run(toggleBold, "one «two» three")).toBe("one **«two»** three");
56:     expect(run(toggleBold, "one «**two**» three")).toBe("one «two» three");
57:   });
58: 
59:   it("removes marks that sit just outside the selection", () => {
60:     expect(run(toggleBold, "one **«two»** three")).toBe("one «two» three");
61:   });
62: 
63:   it("leaves a caret between fresh marks", () => {
64:     expect(run(toggleBold, "one |")).toBe("one **|**");
65:   });
66: 
67:   it("does not mistake half of ** for italic", () => {
68:     expect(run(toggleItalic, "one **«two»** three")).toBe("one ***«two»*** three");
69:   });
70: });
71: 
72: describe("headings", () => {
73:   it("adds and removes the same level", () => {
74:     expect(run(toggleHeading(1), "title|")).toBe("# title|");

79:     expect(run(toggleHeading(2), "# title|")).toBe("## title|");
80:   });

85:     expect(run(cycleList, "item|")).toBe("- item|");
86:     expect(run(cycleList, "- item|")).toBe("1. item|");
87:     expect(run(cycleList, "1. item|")).toBe("- [ ] item|");
88:     expect(run(cycleList, "- [ ] item|")).toBe("item|");
89:   });
90:

92:     expect(run(cycleList, "- «one\n- two\n- three»")).toBe("1. «one\n2. two\n3. three»");
93:   });
94:

99:   });

105:     expect(run(toggleCheckbox, "- [x] task|")).toBe("- [ ] task|");
106:     expect(run(toggleCheckbox, "- task|")).toBe("- [ ] task|");
107:     expect(run(toggleCheckbox, "task|")).toBe("- [ ] task|");

src/read/FindBar.tsx
1: import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
2: import { matchesChord } from "../app/chords";
3: import { FIND_STEP } from "./events";
4: import { clearPaint, findRanges, paint, scrollRangeIntoView } from "./find";
5: 
6: interface Props {
7:   /** The scroll container; also the tree that gets searched. */
8:   container: HTMLElement | null;
9:   /** Bumped whenever the rendered html changes, to redo the search. */
10:   revision: number;
11:   /** Bumped by every `Ctrl+F`, so a second one re-focuses the field. */
12:   focusToken: number;
13:   onClose: () => void;
14: }
15: 
16: /** 36px strip under the mode bar, same shape as a banner (spec §5.1). */
17: export function FindBar({ container, revision, focusToken, onClose }: Props) {
18:   const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
19:   const [caseSensitive, setCaseSensitive] = useState(false);
20:   const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);
21:   const input = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
22: 
23:   useEffect(() => {
24:     input.current?.focus();
25:     input.current?.select();
26:   }, [focusToken]);
27: 
28:   const ranges = useMemo(
29:     () => (container ? findRanges(container, query, caseSensitive) : []),
30:     // `revision` is the point: the same query over freshly rendered html.
31:     // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
32:     [container, query, caseSensitive, revision],
33:   );
34: 
35:   const at = ranges.length === 0 ? 0 : ((index % ranges.length) + ranges.length) % ranges.length;
36: 
37:   useEffect(() => {
38:     const current = ranges[at] ?? null;
39:     paint(ranges, current);
40:     if (current && container) scrollRangeIntoView(container, current);
41:   }, [ranges, at, container]);
42: 
43:   useEffect(() => clearPaint, []);
44: 
45:   const step = (delta: number) => {
46:     if (ranges.length > 0) setIndex((value) => value + delta);
47:   };
48: 
49:   // F3 / Shift+F3 come from the command registry and work whether or not the
50:   // field has focus; `Esc` is the bar's own.
51:   useEffect(() => {
52:     const onStep = (event: Event) => step((event as CustomEvent<1 | -1>).detail);
53:     const onKey = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
54:       if (!matchesChord("Escape", event)) return;
55:       event.preventDefault();
56:       onClose();
57:     };
58:     window.addEventListener(FIND_STEP, onStep);
59:     window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
60:     return () => {
61:       window.removeEventListener(FIND_STEP, onStep);
62:       window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
63:     };
64:   });
65: 
66:   return (
67:     <div className="findbar">
68:       <input
69:         ref={input}
70:         className="find-input"
71:         value={query}
72:         placeholder="find"
73:         spellCheck={false}
74:         onChange={(event) => {
75:           setQuery(event.target.value);
76:           setIndex(0);
77:         }}
78:         onKeyDown={(event) => {
79:           if (event.key !== "Enter") return;
80:           event.preventDefault();
81:           step(event.shiftKey ? -1 : 1);
82:         }}
83:       />
84:       <span className="find-count">
85:         {query === "" ? "" : ranges.length === 0 ? "no matches" : `${at + 1} / ${ranges.length}`}
86:       </span>
87:       <button
88:         className={"find-toggle" + (caseSensitive ? " is-active" : "")}
89:         onClick={() => setCaseSensitive((value) => !value)}
90:         title="match case"
91:       >
92:         Aa
93:       </button>
94:       <button className="link" onClick={onClose}>
95:         close
96:       </button>
97:     </div>
98:   );
99: }
100: 

src/editor/findPanel.ts
1: // Find/replace in the style of the app: a 36px strip under the mode bar,
2: // text controls only, glyph toggles `Aa · W · .*` (spec §5.2).
3: 
4: import {
5:   SearchQuery,
6:   closeSearchPanel,
7:   findNext,
8:   findPrevious,
9:   getSearchQuery,
10:   openSearchPanel,
11:   replaceAll,
12:   replaceNext,
13:   setSearchQuery,
14: } from "@codemirror/search";
15: import type { EditorState } from "@codemirror/state";
16: import type { Command, EditorView, Panel, ViewUpdate } from "@codemirror/view";
17: import { matchesChord } from "../app/chords";
18: 
19: const MAX_COUNT = 2000;
20: 
21: const TOGGLES = ["caseSensitive", "wholeWord", "regexp"] as const;
22: type Toggle = (typeof TOGGLES)[number];
23: 
24: /** `3 / 12`, where the index is the match the selection is sitting on. */
25: function matchInfo(state: EditorState, query: SearchQuery): string {
26:   if (!query.valid) return "";
27:   const selection = state.selection.main;
28:   const cursor = query.getCursor(state);
29:   let total = 0;
30:   let index = 0;
31:   for (let step = cursor.next(); !step.done; step = cursor.next()) {
32:     total += 1;
33:     if (step.value.from === selection.from && step.value.to === selection.to) index = total;
34:     if (total >= MAX_COUNT) return `${index} / ${MAX_COUNT}+`;
35:   }
36:   return `${index} / ${total}`;
37: }
38: 
39: function element<K extends keyof HTMLElementTagNameMap>(
40:   tag: K,
41:   className: string,
42:   text?: string,
43: ): HTMLElementTagNameMap[K] {
44:   const node = document.createElement(tag);
45:   node.className = className;
46:   if (text !== undefined) node.textContent = text;
47:   return node;
48: }
49: 
50: class FindPanel implements Panel {
51:   readonly dom: HTMLElement;
52:   readonly top = true;
53:   private readonly find: HTMLInputElement;
54:   private readonly replace: HTMLInputElement;
55:   private readonly count: HTMLElement;
56:   private readonly toggles: Record<Toggle, HTMLElement>;
57:   private readonly replaceRow: HTMLElement;
58: 
59:   constructor(private readonly view: EditorView) {
60:     const query = getSearchQuery(view.state);
61: 
62:     this.find = element("input", "find-field");
63:     this.find.placeholder = "find";
64:     this.find.value = query.search;
65:     this.find.setAttribute("main-field", "true");
66: 
67:     this.replace = element("input", "find-field");
68:     this.replace.placeholder = "replace";
69:     this.replace.value = query.replace;
70: 
71:     this.count = element("span", "find-count");
72:     this.toggles = {
73:       caseSensitive: element("button", "find-toggle", "Aa"),
74:       wholeWord: element("button", "find-toggle", "W"),
75:       regexp: element("button", "find-toggle", ".*"),
76:     };
77: 
78:     const replaceButton = element("button", "link", "replace");
79:     const replaceAllButton = element("button", "link", "all");
80:     this.replaceRow = element("span", "find-replace");
81:     this.replaceRow.append(this.replace, replaceButton, replaceAllButton);
82:     this.replaceRow.hidden = true;
83: 
84:     this.dom = element("div", "find");
85:     this.dom.append(
86:       element("span", "find-glyph", "⌕"),
87:       this.find,
88:       this.toggles.caseSensitive,
89:       this.toggles.wholeWord,
90:       this.toggles.regexp,
91:       this.count,
92:       this.replaceRow,
93:       element("span", "find-hint", "esc"),
94:     );
95: 
96:     this.find.addEventListener("input", () => this.commit());
97:     this.replace.addEventListener("input", () => this.commit());
98:     for (const key of TOGGLES) {
99:       this.toggles[key].addEventListener("click", () => {
100:         this.commit({ [key]: !getSearchQuery(this.view.state)[key] });
101:       });
102:     }
103:     replaceButton.addEventListener("click", () => replaceNext(this.view));
104:     replaceAllButton.addEventListener("click", () => replaceAll(this.view));
105:     this.dom.addEventListener("keydown", (event) => this.onKey(event));
106: 
107:     this.render();
108:   }
109: 
110:   private onKey(event: KeyboardEvent): void {
111:     if (matchesChord("Ctrl+H", event)) {
112:       event.preventDefault();
113:       this.showReplace();
114:     } else if (event.key === "Escape") {
115:       event.preventDefault();
116:       closeSearchPanel(this.view);
117:       this.view.focus();
118:     } else if (event.key === "Enter") {
119:       event.preventDefault();
120:       if (event.target === this.replace) replaceNext(this.view);
121:       else if (event.shiftKey) findPrevious(this.view);
122:       else findNext(this.view);
123:     } else if (event.key === "F3") {
124:       event.preventDefault();
125:       if (event.shiftKey) findPrevious(this.view);
126:       else findNext(this.view);
127:     }
128:   }
129: 
130:   /** Pushes the fields into the editor's search state. */
131:   private commit(patch: Partial<Record<Toggle, boolean>> = {}): void {
132:     const current = getSearchQuery(this.view.state);
133:     const query = new SearchQuery({
134:       search: this.find.value,
135:       replace: this.replace.value,
136:       caseSensitive: patch["caseSensitive"] ?? current.caseSensitive,
137:       wholeWord: patch["wholeWord"] ?? current.wholeWord,
138:       regexp: patch["regexp"] ?? current.regexp,
139:     });
140:     this.view.dispatch({ effects: setSearchQuery.of(query) });
141:   }
142: 
143:   private render(): void {
144:     const query = getSearchQuery(this.view.state);
145:     this.toggles.caseSensitive.classList.toggle("is-on", query.caseSensitive);
146:     this.toggles.wholeWord.classList.toggle("is-on", query.wholeWord);
147:     this.toggles.regexp.classList.toggle("is-on", query.regexp);
148:     this.count.textContent = matchInfo(this.view.state, query);
149:   }
150: 
151:   showReplace(): void {
152:     this.replaceRow.hidden = false;
153:     this.replace.focus();
154:     this.replace.select();
155:   }
156: 
157:   mount(): void {
158:     this.render();
159:     this.find.select();
160:   }
161: 
162:   update(update: ViewUpdate): void {
163:     const queryChanged = update.transactions.some((tr) =>
164:       tr.effects.some((effect) => effect.is(setSearchQuery)),
165:     );
166:     if (update.docChanged || update.selectionSet || queryChanged) this.render();
167:   }
168: }
169: 
170: const panels = new WeakMap<EditorView, FindPanel>();
171: 
172: export function findPanel(view: EditorView): Panel {
173:   const panel = new FindPanel(view);
174:   panels.set(view, panel);
175:   return panel;
176: }
177: 
178: /** Ctrl+H: the same panel with the replace field showing. */
179: export const openReplace: Command = (view) => {
180:   openSearchPanel(view);
181:   panels.get(view)?.showReplace();
182:   return true;
183: };
184:

node_modules/hast-util-sanitize/lib/schema.js
1: /**
2:  * @import {Schema} from 'hast-util-sanitize'
3:  */
4: 
5: // Couple of ARIA attributes allowed in several, but not all, places.
6: const aria = ['ariaDescribedBy', 'ariaLabel', 'ariaLabelledBy']
7: 
8: /**
9:  * Default schema.
10:  *
11:  * Follows GitHub style sanitation.
12:  *
13:  * @type {Schema}
14:  */
15: export const defaultSchema = {
16:   ancestors: {
17:     tbody: ['table'],
18:     td: ['table'],
19:     th: ['table'],
20:     thead: ['table'],
21:     tfoot: ['table'],
22:     tr: ['table']
23:   },
24:   attributes: {
25:     a: [
26:       ...aria,
27:       // Note: these 3 are used by GFM footnotes, they do work on all links.
28:       'dataFootnoteBackref',
29:       'dataFootnoteRef',
30:       ['className', 'data-footnote-backref'],
31:       'href'
32:     ],
33:     blockquote: ['cite'],
34:     // Note: this class is not normally allowed by GH, when manually writing
35:     // `code` as HTML in markdown, they adds it some other way.
36:     // We can’t do that, so we have to allow it.
37:     code: [['className', /^language-./]],
38:     del: ['cite'],
39:     div: ['itemScope', 'itemType'],
40:     dl: [...aria],
41:     // Note: this is used by GFM footnotes.
42:     h2: [['className', 'sr-only']],
43:     img: [...aria, 'longDesc', 'src'],
44:     // Note: `input` is not normally allowed by GH, when manually writing
45:     // it in markdown, they add it from tasklists some other way.
46:     // We can’t do that, so we have to allow it.
47:     input: [
48:       ['disabled', true],
49:       ['type', 'checkbox']
50:     ],
51:     ins: ['cite'],
52:     // Note: this class is not normally allowed by GH, when manually writing
53:     // `li` as HTML in markdown, they adds it some other way.
54:     // We can’t do that, so we have to allow it.
55:     li: [['className', 'task-list-item']],
56:     // Note: this class is not normally allowed by GH, when manually writing
57:     // `ol` as HTML in markdown, they adds it some other way.
58:     // We can’t do that, so we have to allow it.
59:     ol: [...aria, ['className', 'contains-task-list']],
60:     q: ['cite'],
61:     section: ['dataFootnotes', ['className', 'footnotes']],
62:     source: ['srcSet'],

node_modules/@codemirror/view/dist/index.js
4545:         // On Safari adding an input event handler somehow prevents an
4546:         // issue where the composition vanishes when you press enter.
4547:         if (browser.safari)
4548:             view.contentDOM.addEventListener("input", () => null);
4549:         if (browser.gecko)
4550:             firefoxCopyCutHack(view.contentDOM.ownerDocument);
4551:     }
4552:     handleEvent(event) {
4553:         if (!eventBelongsToEditor(this.view, event) || this.ignoreDuringComposition(event))
4554:             return;
4555:         if (event.type == "keydown" && this.keydown(event))
4556:             return;
4557:         if (this.view.updateState != 0 /* UpdateState.Idle */)
4558:             Promise.resolve().then(() => this.runHandlers(event.type, event));
4559:         else
4560:             this.runHandlers(event.type, event);
4561:     }
4562:     runHandlers(type, event) {
4563:         let handlers = this.handlers[type];
4564:         if (handlers) {
4565:             for (let observer of handlers.observers)
4566:                 observer(this.view, event);
4567:             for (let handler of handlers.handlers) {
4568:                 if (event.defaultPrevented)
4569:                     break;
4570:                 if (handler(this.view, event)) {
4571:                     event.preventDefault();
4572:                     break;
4573:                 }
4574:             }
4575:         }
4576:     }
4577:     ensureHandlers(plugins) {
4578:         let handlers = computeHandlers(plugins), prev = this.handlers, dom = this.view.contentDOM;
4579:         for (let type in handlers)
4580:             if (type != "scroll") {
4581:                 let passive = !handlers[type].handlers.length;
4582:                 let exists = prev[type];
4583:                 if (exists && passive != !exists.handlers.length) {
4584:                     dom.removeEventListener(type, this.handleEvent);
4585:                     exists = null;
4586:                 }
4587:                 if (!exists)
4588:                     dom.addEventListener(type, this.handleEvent, { passive });
4589:             }
4590:         for (let type in prev)
4591:             if (type != "scroll" && !handlers[type])
4592:                 dom.removeEventListener(type, this.handleEvent);
4593:         this.handlers = handlers;
4594:     }
4595:     keydown(event) {

src/read/find.ts
1: // Text search over the rendered document (spec §5.1). No mapping back into
2: // the source: this searches what is on screen, and paints with the CSS
3: // Custom Highlight API so the DOM is never touched.
4: 
5: interface Chunk {
6:   node: Text;
7:   start: number;
8: }
9: 
10: interface Index {
11:   text: string;
12:   chunks: Chunk[];
13: }
14: 
15: /** All visible text of `root`, plus where each text node starts in it. */
16: export function indexText(root: HTMLElement): Index {
17:   const walker = document.createTreeWalker(root, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT, {
18:     acceptNode(node) {
19:       const parent = node.parentElement;
20:       if (!parent || parent.closest("[hidden], .code-bar, .h-anchor")) {
21:         return NodeFilter.FILTER_REJECT;
22:       }
23:       return NodeFilter.FILTER_ACCEPT;
24:     },
25:   });
26: 
27:   const chunks: Chunk[] = [];
28:   let text = "";
29:   let node = walker.nextNode();
30:   while (node) {
31:     const value = node.nodeValue ?? "";
32:     if (value !== "") {
33:       chunks.push({ node: node as Text, start: text.length });
34:       text += value;
35:     }
36:     node = walker.nextNode();
37:   }
38:   return { text, chunks };
39: }
40: 
41: function locate(chunks: Chunk[], offset: number): { node: Text; offset: number } | null {
42:   let low = 0;
43:   let high = chunks.length - 1;
44:   while (low <= high) {
45:     const mid = (low + high) >> 1;
46:     const chunk = chunks[mid];
47:     if (!chunk) break;
48:     const end = chunk.start + (chunk.node.nodeValue?.length ?? 0);
49:     if (offset < chunk.start) high = mid - 1;
50:     else if (offset >= end) low = mid + 1;
51:     else return { node: chunk.node, offset: offset - chunk.start };
52:   }
53:   const last = chunks[chunks.length - 1];
54:   if (last && offset === last.start + (last.node.nodeValue?.length ?? 0)) {
55:     return { node: last.node, offset: last.node.nodeValue?.length ?? 0 };
56:   }
57:   return null;
58: }
59: 
60: /** Ranges for every occurrence of `query`, in document order. */
61: export function findRanges(
62:   root: HTMLElement,
63:   query: string,
64:   caseSensitive: boolean,
65: ): Range[] {
66:   if (query === "") return [];
67:   const { text, chunks } = indexText(root);
68:   const haystack = caseSensitive ? text : text.toLowerCase();
69:   const needle = caseSensitive ? query : query.toLowerCase();
70: 
71:   const ranges: Range[] = [];
72:   let at = haystack.indexOf(needle);
73:   while (at >= 0 && ranges.length < 2000) {
74:     const from = locate(chunks, at);
75:     const to = locate(chunks, at + needle.length);
76:     if (from && to) {
77:       const range = document.createRange();
78:       range.setStart(from.node, from.offset);
79:       range.setEnd(to.node, to.offset);
80:       ranges.push(range);
81:     }
82:     at = haystack.indexOf(needle, at + Math.max(1, needle.length));
83:   }
84:   return ranges;
85: }
86: 
87: /* ------------------------------------------------- CSS Custom Highlight API */
88: 
89: interface HighlightRegistryLike {
90:   set(name: string, value: object): void;
91:   delete(name: string): void;
92: }
93: 
94: const registry = (CSS as unknown as { highlights?: HighlightRegistryLike }).highlights;
95: const HighlightCtor = (globalThis as unknown as { Highlight?: new (...ranges: Range[]) => object })
96:   .Highlight;
97: 
98: export const ALL = "plain-find";
99: export const CURRENT = "plain-find-active";
100: 
101: export function paint(all: Range[], current: Range | null): void {
102:   if (!registry || !HighlightCtor) return;
103:   if (all.length === 0) registry.delete(ALL);
104:   else registry.set(ALL, new HighlightCtor(...all));
105:   if (current) registry.set(CURRENT, new HighlightCtor(current));
106:   else registry.delete(CURRENT);
107: }
108: 
109: export function clearPaint(): void {
110:   registry?.delete(ALL);
111:   registry?.delete(CURRENT);
112: }
113: 
114: /** Scrolls `range` into the middle of `container` when it is out of sight. */
115: export function scrollRangeIntoView(container: HTMLElement, range: Range): void {
116:   const box = range.getBoundingClientRect();
117:   if (box.width === 0 && box.height === 0) return;
118:   const frame = container.getBoundingClientRect();
119:   if (box.top >= frame.top + 8 && box.bottom <= frame.bottom - 8) return;
120:   container.scrollTop += box.top - frame.top - frame.height / 3;
121: }

112: 
113: /// Hands a folder to the asset protocol so its images can be shown, and to
114: /// the file-system scope so links inside it can be opened (spec §9).
115: /// Called by the frontend for the folder of the open file, for the library,
116: /// and for whatever an `outside folder · allow` placeholder points at.
117: #[tauri::command]
118: fn allow_asset_dir(app: tauri::AppHandle, path: String) -> Result<(), String> {
119:     let dir = Path::new(&path);
120:     if !dir.is_dir() {
121:         return Err(format!("not a folder: {path}"));
122:     }
123:     app.asset_protocol_scope()
124:         .allow_directory(dir, true)
125:         .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
126:     if let Some(scope) = app.try_fs_scope() {
127:         scope
128:             .allow_directory(dir, true)
129:             .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
130:     }
131:     Ok(())
132: }
133: 
134: /// The webview never leaves our own origin (spec §14); links open in the
135: /// system browser instead.
136: fn navigation_guard<R: Runtime>() -> TauriPlugin<R> {
137:     PluginBuilder::new("plain-navigation")

ТЗ-Plain.md
113: - **Кодировки.** Чтение: BOM → UTF-8 → `chardetng`. Запись: в кодировке файла, если это UTF-8 или UTF-16 (BOM как был); legacy → при первом сохранении правок `convert to UTF-8 · save as… · cancel`. EOL — доминирующий стиль файла (новые — из `files.newFileEol`, по умолчанию CRLF); смешанный → нормализация с сообщением. `utf-8` в статус-баре кликабельно → `reopen as… (utf-8 · cp1251 · utf-16)`.
114: - **Внешние изменения.** Нет локальных правок → тихая перезагрузка, скролл на тот же заголовок, статус `reloaded from disk`, одна точка undo. Есть правки → баннер `file changed on disk · reload · save my copy as…` (`reload` — с подтверждением потери правок; diff — кандидат v0.2). Проверка внешней версии — по watcher и дополнительно при возврате фокуса окну.
115: - **Черновики.** Любое изменение буфера → `%APPDATA%\Plain\drafts\<id>.json` (id — хэш пути или id безымянного буфера): текст, путь, `baseHash`, кодировка, BOM, EOL, каретка. Debounce 500 мс, при непрерывном вводе — не реже раза в 2 с; запись temp+rename. Удаляется только после подтверждённого сохранения этого текста. Ошибка записи черновика — постоянный баннер `can't write recovery draft`. При старте с непустым `drafts/` — экран `unsaved work found`: `restore · discard` (discard — в корзину).
116: - **Сессия** — `state.json` (открытые файлы, активный, режимы, скролл/каретка, окно, рейка, последняя папка, недавние 20, позиции чтения — LRU 300) с debounce 2 с и при закрытии.
117: - **Большие файлы.** > 2 МБ — подсветка выключена, read по кнопке `render anyway`; статус `large file`. Ничего не обрезается молча.
118: - **Snapshots** `[v0.2]` — копия файла в `%APPDATA%\Plain\history\<id>\<ts>.md` при `Ctrl+S` не чаще раза в 5 мин, 30 дней; `> version history` → список → `restore`.
119: 
120: ---
121: 
122: ## 9. Локальные картинки и ссылки `[v0.1]`
123: 
124: Локальные картинки — Tauri asset protocol; scope выдаётся динамически на папку библиотеки или папку открытого файла. Картинка вне scope (`../images/x.png` у одиночного файла) → плашка `outside folder · allow` (добавляет её папку в scope на сессию). Внешние картинки — плашка с хостом и `load`. Изменение картинки на диске не отслеживается (перерисовка — `F5`). Вставка картинок из буфера и DnD в текст — v0.2; пока — сохранить PNG в `assets/` руками и написать `![](assets/x.png)`.
125:

<div class="dv-thd"><a class="dv-tid" href="#t1">1</a><span class="dv-tname">Plain — a monospace markdown reader/editor for Windows</span></div>
<p class="dv-notes"><b>Assumptions.</b> Windows 11 window chrome (thin title bar, right-aligned controls). No attached design system content, so the direction is set here: IBM Plex Mono throughout, warm paper light theme, soot dark theme, one accent (violet) used only for the caret, selection and the active item. No icons except where a glyph is the label. Three surfaces: Read, Edit (raw markdown), Rich (WYSIWYG-ish). The library lives in a collapsible left rail. Sample document is a short note about plain text.</p>

          <h1 style="font-size:22px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.3;margin:0 0 26px;letter-spacing:-.01em">On plain text</h1>
          <p style="margin:0 0 20px">Plain text is the only format that has survived every platform, every editor and every company that tried to own it. A note written in 1985 opens today without ceremony.</p>
          <p style="margin:0 0 20px">Markdown adds just enough structure to stay readable in both directions: as source, and as the thing the source describes. The syntax is the formatting.</p>
          <blockquote style="margin:0 0 20px;padding:0 0 0 18px;border-left:1px solid #1c1b19;color:#5c5a54;font-style:italic">The best interface for a paragraph is the paragraph.</blockquote>
          <h2 style="font-size:13.5px;font-weight:600;margin:30px 0 14px;color:#8a877f">Three rules</h2>
          <ol style="margin:0 0 20px;padding-left:22px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px"><li>One file per idea.</li><li>Headings are the outline; never nest deeper than two.</li><li>Links go to other files, not to the web.</li></ol>

<span style="color:#7a776f">#</span> <span style="font-weight:600">On plain text</span>

Plain text is the only format that has survived every platform, every editor and every company that tried to own it. A note written in 1985 opens today without ceremony.

Markdown adds just enough structure to stay readable in both directions: as source, and as the thing the source describes. The syntax <span style="background:rgba(167,139,250,.28)">is the formatting.</span>{{ caret }}

<span style="color:#7a776f">&gt;</span> <span style="font-style:italic">The best interface for a paragraph is the paragraph.</span>

<span style="color:#7a776f">##</span> <span style="font-weight:600">Three rules</span>

<span style="color:#7a776f">1.</span> One file per idea.
<span style="color:#7a776f">2.</span> Headings are the outline; never nest deeper than two.
<span style="color:#7a776f">3.</span> Links go to <span style="color:#7a776f">[</span>other files<span style="color:#7a776f">](</span><span style="color:#a78bfa;text-decoration:underline">ideas.md</span><span style="color:#7a776f">)</span>, not to the web.</div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div style="height:30px;flex:none;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;padding:0 20px;font-size:10.5px;color:#7a776f;border-top:1px solid #262523"><span>ln 9, col 118 · markdown</span><span style="display:flex;gap:16px"><span>utf-8</span><span>612 words</span><span>unsaved</span></span></div>
  </div>
</div></div>

// %APPDATA%\Plain\drafts\<id>.json — the copy of an edited buffer that
// survives a kill (spec §8). Written on a 500 ms debounce and at least once
// every 2 s of continuous typing; deleted only when that same text has
// reached the disk, or when the user says `don't save`.

import { appDataDir, join } from "@tauri-apps/api/path";
import { exists, readDir, readTextFile, remove } from "@tauri-apps/plugin-fs";
import { isDirty } from "../editor/buffers";
import { normalizeEol, type Eol } from "./eol";
import { inTauri } from "./env";
import { readFile, trashPath, writeTextAtomic } from "./fs";
import { basename, pathKey } from "./paths";
import { makeDoc, useStore, type Doc, type Recovery } from "./store";

const FOLDER = "drafts";
export const DEBOUNCE_MS = 500;
export const MAX_WAIT_MS = 2000;

export interface Draft {
  text: string;
  path: string | null;
  title: string;
  baseHash: string | null;
  encoding: string;
  bom: boolean;
  eol: Eol;
  finalNewline: boolean;
  caret: { line: number; col: number } | null;
  savedAt: number;
}

/** Short, stable and derived from the path — the same file always lands on
 *  the same draft, run after run. */
function fnv1a(text: string): string {
  let hash = 0x811c9dc5;
  for (let index = 0; index < text.length; index += 1) {
    hash ^= text.charCodeAt(index);
    hash = Math.imul(hash, 0x01000193);
  }
  return (hash >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

export function draftName(doc: Pick<Doc, "id" | "path">): string {
  return `${fnv1a(doc.path ? pathKey(doc.path) : doc.id)}.json`;
}

async function draftPath(doc: Pick<Doc, "id" | "path">): Promise<string> {
  return join(await appDataDir(), FOLDER, draftName(doc));
}

/* --------------------------------------------------------------- writing */

/**
 * Debounce with a ceiling: quiet for 500 ms writes, and typing that never
 * pauses still gets written every 2 s (spec §8).
 */
export function createSchedule(run: () => void): { push: () => void; flush: () => void } {
  let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
  let firstAt = 0;

  const flush = () => {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    timer = undefined;
    firstAt = 0;
    run();
  };

  return {
    push() {
      const now = Date.now();
      if (firstAt === 0) firstAt = now;
      if (now - firstAt >= MAX_WAIT_MS) {
        flush();
        return;
      }
      clearTimeout(timer);
      timer = setTimeout(flush, DEBOUNCE_MS);
    },
    flush,
  };
}

const pending = new Set<string>();

const schedule = createSchedule(() => {
  const ids = [...pending];
  pending.clear();
  for (const id of ids) void writeDraft(id);
});

export function scheduleDraft(id: string): void {
  pending.add(id);
  schedule.push();
}

export function flushDrafts(): void {
  schedule.flush();
}

/** Tests only. */
export function pendingDrafts(): string[] {
  return [...pending];
}

export async function writeDraft(id: string): Promise<void> {
  if (!inTauri) return;
  const doc = useStore.getState().docs.find((d) => d.id === id);
  if (!doc || !isDirty(doc.id, doc.text)) return;

  const draft: Draft = {
    text: normalizeEol(doc.text),
    path: doc.path,
    title: doc.title,
    baseHash: doc.baseHash,
    encoding: doc.encoding,
    bom: doc.bom,
    eol: doc.eol,
    finalNewline: doc.finalNewline,
    caret: doc.caret,
    savedAt: Date.now(),
  };
  try {
    await writeTextAtomic(await draftPath(doc), JSON.stringify(draft));
    useStore.getState().dismissBanner("drafts");
  } catch {
    // Nothing to offer here: the user cannot fix %APPDATA% from inside the
    // app, and the banner has to stay up as long as the risk does (§8).
    useStore.getState().showBanner({ id: "drafts", text: "can't write recovery draft" });
  }
}

export async function dropDraft(doc: Pick<Doc, "id" | "path">): Promise<void> {
  if (!inTauri) return;
  pending.delete(doc.id);
  try {
    const file = await draftPath(doc);
    if (await exists(file)) await remove(file);
  } catch {
    /* a draft that will not go away is not worth a banner */
  }
}

/** Every dirty buffer belongs to the store, so one subscription covers all. */
export function installDrafts(): () => void {
  return useStore.subscribe((state, previous) => {
    if (state.docs === previous.docs) return;
    for (const doc of state.docs) {
      const before = previous.docs.find((d) => d.id === doc.id);
      if (before && before.text === doc.text && before.baseHash === doc.baseHash) continue;
      if (isDirty(doc.id, doc.text)) scheduleDraft(doc.id);
    }
  });
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------ recovering */

async function loadDraft(file: string): Promise<Draft | null> {
  try {
    const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(await readTextFile(file));
    if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== "object") return null;
    const draft = parsed as Draft;
    return typeof draft.text === "string" ? draft : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/** What the `unsaved work found` screen lists at startup (spec §8). */
export async function listDrafts(): Promise<Recovery[]> {
  if (!inTauri) return [];
  try {
    const folder = await join(await appDataDir(), FOLDER);
    if (!(await exists(folder))) return [];
    const found: Recovery[] = [];
    for (const entry of await readDir(folder)) {
      if (!entry.isFile || !entry.name.endsWith(".json")) continue;
      const file = await join(folder, entry.name);
      const draft = await loadDraft(file);
      if (!draft) continue;
      found.push({
        file,
        path: draft.path,
        title: draft.title || (draft.path ? basename(draft.path) : "Untitled"),
        savedAt: draft.savedAt ?? 0,
      });
    }
    return found.sort((a, b) => b.savedAt - a.savedAt);
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

/**
 * Opens the draft as an unsaved document. Says whether the file on disk has
 * moved on since — the caller raises the conflict banner, so this module does
 * not have to know about banners.
 */
export async function restoreDraft(entry: Recovery): Promise<{ id: string; conflict: boolean } | null> {
  const draft = await loadDraft(entry.file);
  if (!draft) return null;

  const text = normalizeEol(draft.text);
  let savedText = "";
  let readOnly = false;
  let deleted = false;
  let conflict = false;

  if (draft.path) {
    try {
      const info = await readFile(draft.path);
      savedText = normalizeEol(info.text);
      readOnly = info.readOnly;
      conflict = info.hash !== draft.baseHash;
    } catch {
      deleted = true;
    }
  }

  const id = draft.path ? pathKey(draft.path) : entry.file;
  const doc = makeDoc({
    id,
    path: draft.path,
    title: entry.title,
    text,
    savedText,
    dirty: text !== savedText,
    mode: "edit",
    readOnly,
    deleted,
    encoding: draft.encoding ?? "utf-8",
    bom: draft.bom ?? false,
    eol: draft.eol ?? "crlf",
    finalNewline: draft.finalNewline ?? false,
    baseHash: draft.baseHash ?? null,
    caret: draft.caret ?? null,
  });
  useStore.getState().openDoc(doc);
  return { id, conflict };
}

/** `discard` goes to the Recycle Bin, not to nowhere (spec §8). */
export async function discardDraft(entry: Recovery): Promise<void> {
  if (!inTauri) return;
  try {
    await trashPath(entry.file);
  } catch {
    try {
      await remove(entry.file);
    } catch {
      /* nothing else to try */
    }
  }
}

//#region src/tinykeys.ts
/**
* These are the modifier keys that change the meaning of keybindings.
*
* Note: Ignoring "AltGraph" because it is covered by the others.
*/
let KEYBINDING_MODIFIER_KEYS = [
	"Shift",
	"Meta",
	"Alt",
	"Control"
];
/**
* Keybinding sequences should timeout if individual key presses are more than
* 1s apart by default.
*/
let DEFAULT_TIMEOUT = 1e3;
/**
* Keybinding sequences should bind to this event by default.
*/
let DEFAULT_EVENT = "keydown";
/**
* Platform detection code.
* @see https://github.com/jamiebuilds/tinykeys/issues/184
*/
let PLATFORM = typeof navigator === "object" ? navigator.platform : "";
/**
* An alias for creating platform-specific keybinding aliases.
*/
let MOD = /Mac|iPod|iPhone|iPad/.test(PLATFORM) ? "Meta" : "Control";
/**
* Meaning of `AltGraph`, from MDN:
* - Windows: Both Alt and Ctrl keys are pressed, or AltGr key is pressed
* - Mac: ⌥ Option key pressed
* - Linux: Level 3 Shift key (or Level 5 Shift key) pressed
* - Android: Not supported
* @see https://github.com/jamiebuilds/tinykeys/issues/185
*/
let ALT_GRAPH_ALIASES = PLATFORM === "Win32" ? ["Control", "Alt"] : ["Alt"];
/**
* Ensure and stop any event that isn't a full keyboard event.
* Autocomplete option navigation and selection would fire an Event,
* instead of the expected KeyboardEvent
*/
function isKeyboardEvent(event) {
	return !!(event.key && event.code && event.getModifierState);
}
/**
* Ignores keyboard events from contenteditable and form elements unless they
* are the current target.
*/
function defaultKeybindingsHandlerIgnore(event) {
	let target = event.target;
	return event.repeat || event.isComposing || target !== event.currentTarget && target.matches("[contenteditable],input,select,textarea");
}
/**
* There's a bug in Chrome that causes event.getModifierState not to exist on
* KeyboardEvent's for F1/F2/etc keys.
*/
function getModifierState(event, mod) {
	return typeof event.getModifierState === "function" ? event.getModifierState(mod) || ALT_GRAPH_ALIASES.includes(mod) && event.getModifierState("AltGraph") : false;
}
/**
* Parses a keybinding string into its parts.
*
* ```
* grammar    = `<sequence>`
* <sequence> = `<press> <press> <press> ...`
* <press>    = `<key>` or `<mods>+<key>`
* <mods>     = `<mod>+<mod>+...`
* <mod>      = `<modifier>` (required) or `[<modifier>]` (optional)
* <key>      = `<KeyboardEvent.key>` or `<KeyboardEvent.code>` (case-insensitive)
* <key>      = `(<regex>)` -> `/^(?:<regex>)$/iy` (case-insensitive)
* ```
*/
function parseKeybinding(str) {
	return str.trim().split(" ").map((press) => {
		let parts = press.split(/(?<=\w|\])\+/);
		let last = parts.pop();
		let regex = last.match(/^\((.+)\)$/);
		let key = regex ? new RegExp(`^(?:${regex[1]})$`, "iv") : last;
		let requiredModifiers = [];
		let optionalModifiers = [];
		for (const part of parts) {
			let optional = part.match(/^\[(.*)\]$/);
			let mod = optional?.[1] ?? part;
			mod = mod === "$mod" ? MOD : mod;
			if (optional) optionalModifiers.push(mod);
			else requiredModifiers.push(mod);
		}
		return [
			requiredModifiers,
			optionalModifiers,
			key
		];
	});
}
/**
* This tells us if a single keyboard event matches a single keybinding press.
*/
function matchKeybindingPress(event, [requiredModifiers, optionalModifiers, key]) {
	const hasAltGraph = requiredModifiers.includes("AltGraph");
	return !((key instanceof RegExp ? !(key.test(event.key) || key.test(event.code)) : key.toUpperCase() !== event.key.toUpperCase() && key !== event.code) || requiredModifiers.find((mod) => {
		return !getModifierState(event, mod);
	}) || KEYBINDING_MODIFIER_KEYS.find((mod) => {
		return !requiredModifiers.includes(mod) && !optionalModifiers.includes(mod) && key !== mod && getModifierState(event, mod) && !(hasAltGraph && ALT_GRAPH_ALIASES.includes(mod));
	}));
}
/**
* Creates an event listener for handling keybindings.
*
* @example
* ```js
* import { createKeybindingsHandler } from "../src/keybindings"
*
* let handler = createKeybindingsHandler({
* 	"Shift+d": () => {
* 		alert("The 'Shift' and 'd' keys were pressed at the same time")
* 	},
* 	"y e e t": () => {
* 		alert("The keys 'y', 'e', 'e', and 't' were pressed in order")
* 	},
* 	"$mod+d": () => {
* 		alert("Either 'Control+d' or 'Meta+d' were pressed")
* 	},
* })
*
* window.addEventListener("keydown", handler)
* ```
*/
function createKeybindingsHandler(keybindingsMap, options = {}) {
	let timeout = options.timeout ?? DEFAULT_TIMEOUT;
	let ignore = options.ignore ?? defaultKeybindingsHandlerIgnore;
	let keybindings = Object.keys(keybindingsMap).map((input) => {
		return [
			input,
			parseKeybinding(input),
			keybindingsMap[input]
		];
	});
	let pending = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
	let timer = null;
	return (event) => {
		if (!isKeyboardEvent(event) || ignore(event)) return;
		let conflicts = [];
		for (let [input, sequence, handler] of keybindings) {
			let prev = pending.get(input);
			let [current, ...rest] = prev ? prev : sequence;
			if (!matchKeybindingPress(event, current)) {
				if (!getModifierState(event, event.key)) pending.delete(input);
			} else if (rest.length > 0) {
				pending.set(input, rest);
				conflicts.push(input);
			} else {
				pending.delete(input);
				if (conflicts.length) console.warn(`tinykeys: Conflict found, "${input}" did not fire, waiting for:`, conflicts);
				else {
					handler(event);
					break;
				}
			}
		}
		if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
		timer = setTimeout(() => pending.clear(), timeout);
	};
}
/**
* Subscribes to keybindings.
*
* Returns an unsubscribe method.
*
* @example
* ```js
* import { tinykeys } from "../src/tinykeys"
*
* tinykeys(window, {
* 	"Shift+d": () => {
* 		alert("The 'Shift' and 'd' keys were pressed at the same time")
* 	},
* 	"y e e t": () => {
* 		alert("The keys 'y', 'e', 'e', and 't' were pressed in order")
* 	},
* 	"$mod+d": () => {
* 		alert("Either 'Control+d' or 'Meta+d' were pressed")
* 	},
* })
* ```
*/
function tinykeys(target, keybindingMap, options = {}) {
	let event = options.event ?? DEFAULT_EVENT;
	let onKeyEvent = createKeybindingsHandler(keybindingMap, options);
	target.addEventListener(event, onKeyEvent, options.capture);
	return () => {
		target.removeEventListener(event, onKeyEvent, options.capture);
	};
}
//#endregion
export { createKeybindingsHandler, defaultKeybindingsHandlerIgnore, matchKeybindingPress, parseKeybinding, tinykeys };

import { Fragment, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { encodingLabel } from "../app/eol";
import { reopenAs } from "../app/save";
import { countWords } from "../read/words";
import { activeDoc, useStore, type Doc } from "../app/store";
import "./dialogs.css";

/** Spec §4, in the order the spec lists them. */
function state(doc: Doc): string {
  if (doc.deleted) return "deleted on disk";
  if (doc.readOnly) return "read-only";
  if (doc.dirty) return "unsaved";
  if (doc.large) return "large file";
  return "saved";
}

const REOPEN = ["utf-8", "cp1251", "utf-16"];
/** What the status bar calls an encoding vs what Rust wants back. */
const LABELS: Record<string, string> = { cp1251: "windows-1251", "utf-16": "utf-16le" };

export function StatusBar() {
  const message = useStore((s) => s.message);
  const note = useStore((s) => s.note);
  const doc = useStore(activeDoc);
  const resolvedTheme = useStore((s) => s.resolvedTheme);
  const toggleTheme = useStore((s) => s.toggleTheme);
  const [menu, setMenu] = useState(false);

  // The menu is a click away from everything else, so anything else closes it.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!menu) return;
    const close = () => setMenu(false);
    window.addEventListener("mousedown", close);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("mousedown", close);
  }, [menu]);

  useEffect(() => setMenu(false), [doc?.id]);

  // Messages win for their three seconds; otherwise edit shows the caret.
  const caret =
    doc?.mode === "edit" && doc.caret ? `ln ${doc.caret.line}, col ${doc.caret.col}` : null;
  const left = message ?? caret;

  // Wave 3 changes the text on every keystroke, so keep this off that path.
  const text = doc?.text ?? "";
  const words = useMemo(() => countWords(text), [text]);

  return (
    <div className="statusbar">
      <span className="status-left">{left}</span>
      <span className="status-right">
        {doc && (
          <span className="status-encoding">
            <button
              className="link"
              title="reopen as…"
              onMouseDown={(event) => {
                event.stopPropagation();
                setMenu((open) => !open);
              }}
            >
              {encodingLabel(doc.encoding)}
            </button>
            {menu && (
              <span className="encoding-menu" onMouseDown={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}>
                <span className="sep">reopen as…</span>
                {REOPEN.map((label, index) => (
                  <Fragment key={label}>
                    {index > 0 && <span className="sep">·</span>}
                    <button
                      className="link"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setMenu(false);
                        reopenAs(LABELS[label] ?? label);
                      }}
                    >
                      {label}
                    </button>
                  </Fragment>
                ))}
              </span>
            )}
          </span>
        )}
        {doc && <span className="status-words">{words.toLocaleString("en-US")} words</span>}
        {doc && <span>{note ?? state(doc)}</span>}
        <button className="link theme-toggle" onClick={toggleTheme}>
          {resolvedTheme === "dark" ? "◑ dark" : "◐ light"}
        </button>
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}

// Alt+left / Alt+right over the documents visited in this session
// (spec §5.1). Ids only — the buffers live in the store.

import { useStore } from "../app/store";

const back: string[] = [];
const forward: string[] = [];
let current: string | null = useStore.getState().activeId;
let moving = false;

useStore.subscribe((state) => {
  const id = state.activeId;
  if (id === current) return;
  if (!moving) {
    if (current) back.push(current);
    forward.length = 0;
  }
  current = id;
});

/** Pops until it finds a document that is still open. */
function step(from: string[], to: string[]): void {
  const store = useStore.getState();
  while (from.length > 0) {
    const id = from.pop();
    if (!id || !store.docs.some((doc) => doc.id === id)) continue;
    if (current) to.push(current);
    moving = true;
    store.activate(id);
    moving = false;
    return;
  }
}

export function goBack(): void {
  step(back, forward);
}

export function goForward(): void {
  step(forward, back);
}

export function App() {
  const railCollapsed = useStore((s) => s.railCollapsed);
  const banner = useStore((s) => s.banner);
  const recovery = useStore((s) => s.recovery);
  const settingsOpen = useStore((s) => s.settingsOpen);
  const folderSearch = useStore((s) => s.folderSearch);
  const searching = useStore((s) => s.quickSearch !== null);
  const doc = useStore(activeDoc);

  useEffect(() => installKeys(), []);
  useEffect(() => installMenu(), []);
  useEffect(() => installDrafts(), []);
  useEffect(() => installSession(), []);
  useEffect(() => installWatcher(), []);
  useEffect(() => installCloseGuard(), []);
  useEffect(() => installTree(), []);
  useEffect(() => installDrop(), []);

  useEffect(
    () => watchSystemTheme((resolved) => useStore.getState().syncSystemTheme(resolved)),
    [],
  );

  // Spec §3: the dirty marker also shows in the window title.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!inTauri) return;
    const title = doc ? `${doc.title}${doc.dirty ? " •" : ""} — Plain` : "Plain";
    void getCurrentWindow().setTitle(title);
  }, [doc]);

  return (
    <div className={"app" + (searching ? " is-searching" : "")}>
      <div className="app-body">
        {!railCollapsed && <Rail />}
        <div className="main">
          <ModeBar />
          {banner && <Banner banner={banner} />}
          {recovery && recovery.length > 0 ? (
            <RecoveryScreen entries={recovery} />
          ) : settingsOpen ? (
            <SettingsScreen />
          ) : folderSearch ? (
            <FolderSearch />
          ) : doc ? (
            <DocView doc={doc} />
          ) : (
            <Empty />
          )}
          <StatusBar />
        </div>
      </div>
      <Modal />
      <QuickSearch />
    </div>
  );
}

codex
Проверка на синтетических файлах около 1 МБ показала 30–31 секунду на синхронный `render()` в Node 22; это не замер живого WebView2, но блокирующий путь требует внимания. Ещё подтвердились проблемы вложенного italic и границ выделения. Завершаю отзыв, оставляя только находки с конкретным сценарием.

codex
Файлы не менял. Ниже — находки по коду; примеры formatting и pipeline дополнительно проверены в Node. Живой WebView2 не автоматизировал.

**Критично**

1. **[sync.ts:60](<G:/Claude Projects/md reader/src/editor/sync.ts:60>) — отложенный текст используется для решения о тихом reload.** До idle-flush `Doc.text` содержит предыдущую версию. Между вводом и flush нажатие F5 либо событие watcher может признать документ чистым: `refresh()` и `checkDoc()` проверяют именно `isDirty(id, doc.text)`. Затем `replaceText()` подменяет актуальный EditorState дисковой версией без подтверждения. Undo может вернуть правку, но пользователь не получает предупреждения, а recovery draft удаляется. **Предложение:** перед решением о reload брать актуальное состояние редактора; после асинхронного чтения повторно проверять revision буфера. Одного flush перед `await` недостаточно.

2. **[pipeline.ts:158](<G:/Claude Projects/md reader/src/read/pipeline.ts:158>) — `source.srcset` обходит запрет автоматической сети.** Проверенный пример `<picture><source srcset="https://example.com/tracker.png"><img></picture>` проходит pipeline без изменений. Откладывается только `img.src`; браузер выбирает изображение из `source.srcset` самостоятельно ([MDN](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTML/Reference/Elements/picture)). CSP разрешает HTTPS-картинки. Аналогично проходит `data:image/svg+xml` в `srcset`, минуя SVG guard. **Предложение:** для текущего scope проекта убрать `source`/`srcSet` из schema; поддержка responsive pictures здесь не окупает дополнительный путь проверки.

**Стоит поправить**

3. **[commands.ts:122](<G:/Claude Projects/md reader/src/editor/commands.ts:122>) — code block не работает как toggle.** Проверено: выделить `abc`, вызвать команду дважды — получится пять строк fences/content вместо исходного текста. После первого вызова выделение остаётся внутри fences, а снятие требует выделить сами fences. При нескольких раздельных selections команда также оборачивает весь промежуток между первым и последним, включая невыделенный текст. **Предложение:** распознавать окружающую пару fences и обрабатывать отдельные непрерывные группы выбранных строк.

4. **[commands.ts:42](<G:/Claude Projects/md reader/src/editor/commands.ts:42>) — italic внутри bold нельзя снять повторным вызовом.** Проверено на выделенном `abc` в `**abc**`: первый `Ctrl+I` даёт `***abc***`, второй — `****abc****`. `partOfBold()` блокирует снятие и у тройной последовательности. **Предложение:** различать двойные и тройные delimiters; добавить round-trip для bold + italic.

5. **[commands.ts:27](<G:/Claude Projects/md reader/src/editor/commands.ts:27>) — затрагивается строка за границей выделения.** Выделение `[0, 4)` в `one\ntwo` возвращает обе строки, хотя из второй не выбран ни один символ. Heading, quote, list и checkbox меняют лишнюю строку. **Предложение:** для непустого range, заканчивающегося в начале строки, считать последней затронутой строкой `lineAt(range.to - 1)`.

6. **[commands.ts:51](<G:/Claude Projects/md reader/src/editor/commands.ts:51>) — собственные команды обходят read-only.** Они не проверяют `state.readOnly`; сам `state.update()` изменения не запрещает. Проверено: `toggleBold` меняет документ с `EditorState.readOnly.of(true)`, хотя обычный ввод заблокирован. То же относится к другим собственным formatting-командам и toolbar. **Предложение:** единый guard для изменяющих команд, применяемый также при вызове через меню.

7. **[ReadView.tsx:245](<G:/Claude Projects/md reader/src/read/ReadView.tsx:245>) — возврат в edit уничтожает сохранённое выделение.** Cleanup всегда отправляет goto-line, а `jump()` заменяет selection единственной кареткой в начале заголовка. Сценарий: выделить текст → read → edit — выделение потеряно, несмотря на сохранённый EditorState. Это расходится с §5.0: требуется сохранить selection и отдельно поставить scroll на заголовок. **Предложение:** разделить «прокрутить к строке» и «перенести каретку».

8. **[Outline.tsx:27](<G:/Claude Projects/md reader/src/ui/Outline.tsx:27>) — outline в edit использует устаревшие строки.** `doc.headings` обновляется только ReadView. После вставки строк над заголовком кнопка ведёт на старый номер; документ, открытый сразу в edit, вообще остаётся без outline. **Предложение:** обновлять позиции заголовков из актуального буфера с debounce либо вычислять актуальную цель при клике. Полный HTML-render на вводе не нужен.

9. **[headings.ts:18](<G:/Claude Projects/md reader/src/editor/headings.ts:18>) — edit и read вычисляют разные heading IDs.** Проверено: `# [[file|Alias]]` получает `alias` в read и `file` в edit; setext-заголовок отсутствует в editor-списке вообще. Поэтому переход edit → read не находит цель. Boolean-переключение fences также не учитывает их тип и длину. **Предложение:** использовать общий источник heading metadata на переходе между режимами; второй упрощённый Markdown parser уже требует больше поддержки, чем экономит.

10. **[ReadView.tsx:183](<G:/Claude Projects/md reader/src/read/ReadView.tsx:183>) — одинаковый HTML разных документов сохраняет чужие картинки.** При переключении между двумя файлами с одинаковым `![](image.png)` HTML не меняется, поэтому DOM не пересоздаётся. Предыдущий `enhance()` уже удалил `data-src` и записал абсолютный путь; повторный проход с новым `dir` такую картинку пропускает. Она продолжает показывать файл из предыдущей папки. **Предложение:** пересоздавать исходный DOM также при смене document identity/base directory.

11. **[FindBar.tsx:28](<G:/Claude Projects/md reader/src/read/FindBar.tsx:28>) — поиск пересчитывается раньше замены DOM.** При открытом find и смене документа `useMemo` ищет в старом содержимом; новое `innerHTML` устанавливается позже, в effect родителя. Полученные Range после этого относятся к удалённым узлам. Дополнительно `nonce + html.length` не различает разные документы одинаковой длины. **Предложение:** пересчитывать поиск после установки и обработки DOM, передавая настоящую revision вместо длины HTML.

12. **[findPanel.ts:123](<G:/Claude Projects/md reader/src/editor/findPanel.ts:123>) — F3 в поле поиска выполняется дважды.** Локальный handler вызывает `findNext`, затем событие доходит до глобального tinykeys, который отправляет `FIND_STEP`; remote listener вызывает `findNext` снова. `preventDefault()` не останавливает propagation, а установленный tinykeys не пропускает такие события. **Предложение:** оставить F3 одному владельцу — глобальному registry — и убрать локальное дублирование.

13. **[setup.ts:177](<G:/Claude Projects/md reader/src/editor/setup.ts:177>) — отсутствует вставка URL поверх выделения.** Paste handler для `[выделение](url)` нигде в editor не подключён. Обычная вставка заменяет выбранный текст адресом, вопреки §5.2. **Предложение:** небольшой paste handler для URL и непустого selection, с отдельным обходом для plain paste и IME.

14. **[ReadView.tsx:94](<G:/Claude Projects/md reader/src/read/ReadView.tsx:94>) — синхронный render способен надолго заблокировать интерфейс.** На синтетических ASCII-файлах около 1 МБ получил примерно 29,7 секунды для повторяющихся Markdown-параграфов и 31,3 секунды для множества коротких code blocks. Это Node 22, не измерение WebView2, но весь этот вызов находится внутри React render. Кроме того, idle-flush запускает полный word count на том же UI thread: перенос за пределы CM update не делает работу неблокирующей. **Предложение:** проверить эти случаи в WebView2; тяжёлый render/count вынести в Worker с revision документа. Оптимизация ограниченного viewport-прохода `lineLook` здесь вторична.

15. **[words.ts:29](<G:/Claude Projects/md reader/src/read/words.ts:29>) — fenced code внутри blockquote считается словами.** Проверено: `> ```\n> many code words\n> ```` даёт три слова, хотя read строит code block. Regex видит только fences верхнего уровня; аналогичная проблема возникает во вложенных списках. **Предложение:** в read считать слова по уже разобранному Markdown tree, исключая code nodes; не расширять отдельный regex-парсер последовательностью исключений.

**Вкусовщина**

16. **[ReadView.tsx:28](<G:/Claude Projects/md reader/src/read/ReadView.tsx:28>) — `scrollTops` не очищается при закрытии документов.** За сессию остаются записи всех прочитанных файлов, включая переименованные пути. Для одного пользователя объём небольшой, отдельный LRU здесь избыточен. **Предложение:** удалять запись при закрытии документа; долговременная позиция уже хранится отдельно.
